Chapter 960: Who Said It Was Three Against One?

“Immortal Fairy, is something the matter?”

“Nothing much. It’s just that this lass Jinghong was speaking to her ring just now. There might be someone inside it.”

Lu Yang’s heart stirred. He felt that he and Jinghong were fellow sufferers sharing the same predicament.

Senior Jinghong likely also has a big-shot cultivator residing within her ring. It’s fine when they’re silent, but the moment they speak, their prestige comes crashing down. They will chatter incessantly, yet prove utterly useless during cultivation. We can only rely on ourselves.

“Immortal Fairy, did Senior Moonchaser use her Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit to make these two divine lords so scared?”

“Not bad, your discernment has reached three-tenths of my level.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy felt immensely gratified. My efforts in meticulously guiding Lu Yang daily haven’t been in vain; it has paid off in honing his discernment to this level.

“This lass Bai Ri is rather quick-witted. She imbued these two with fear from the Seven Emotions. In the eyes of these so-called divine lords, Bai Ri’s image must now be equivalent to that of the person they fear most.

“Why didn’t half-immortals wielding the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit employ such a method?”

“Immortal Fairy, do you know what they want with Bai Ye?”

“Hmm, a deceased moon laurel tree only has the effect of strengthening souls. I reckon someone needs their soul strengthened.

“The two of them seem to hold rather high positions in the Great Qian Dynasty. They’re probably planning to offer it to an immortal from their dynasty.”

“Who is this little lass?” With the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit no longer out of control, the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace disciples ceased their pursuit of Lu Yang and turned their attention to the commotion at the entrance instead.

Seeing the Moonchaser True Person’s petite figure, they initially mistook her for another disciple afflicted with the shrinking poison.

However, they quickly dismissed this possibility. This little lass appears to be even more powerful than Sect Ancestor Fang; she must be some prominent figure.

“Who are you?!” The two divine lords trembled like sieves, the overwhelming fear rendering them incapable of coherent thought.

“You are unworthy to know.” How could the Moonchaser True Person admit that this current tiny form was her?

Noticing Luo Hongxia’s unusual expression, Sect Ancestor Fang secretly transmitted an auditory message. “Little Luo, do you know this senior?”

“She is our Founding Ancestor Moonchaser.”

“What?!” Sect Ancestor Fang was astonished. I’ve seen Founding Ancestor Moonchaser before. In those days, she was elegant and unparalleled, the epitome of beauty in the world. How did she become a tiny tyke? Even falling into qi deviation won’t result in this!

“Don’t ask for specifics. Founding Ancestor Moonchaser forbade me from speaking about it.”

“Oh.”

Just as the two divine lords were about to surrender and concede defeat, a complex and magnificent golden talisman rune suddenly flashed between their brows, instantly restoring their clarity of mind.

“No, we fell for their trap!” Only then did the two divine lords realize that their previous state was far from normal.

“Thankfully, we have the protective talisman rune bestowed by Master."

“Just now, it made you experience sorrow, and now it fills us both with fear. Be careful. The Incipient Dao Fruit our opponent wields is related to emotions. It may be the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit recorded in ancient texts!” Upon returning to normal, the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord immediately analyzed the situation, his guess hitting close to the mark.

This is troublesome. Matters of emotion are intangible and elusive. The Vajra Incipient Dao Fruit I possess is not suited for dealing with this type of Incipient Dao Fruit.

Neither can I rely on the protective talisman rune bestowed by the Emperor-Lord every time. Sooner or later, its uses would be exhausted.

“There are a few cultivators of later generations with some skills after all.”

Having emerged from the chaotic period of the early Great Qian era, the two divine lords were powerful figures and immediately came up with a countermeasure.

“Thus have I heard: the Vajra cannot be destroyed by any dharma, yet can destroy all things; so too is this wisdom...”

The two divine lords simultaneously recited Buddhist scriptures, subduing their restless minds and rectifying their hearts. Though this couldn’t completely resist the influence of the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruits, it would greatly abate it.

At the very least, it prevented them from being too terrified of the Moonchaser True Person to fight.

“Let me assist you, Senior!” Sect Ancestors Fang pretended to be clueless about the Moonchaser True Person’s identity.

“Vajra Vanquishes Devils!” A layer of golden radiance emerged from the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord, with a red dot between his brows. He resembled a supreme arhat from the western Buddhist Kingdom. With three heads and six arms, he wielded six vajra scepters. Of his two additional faces, one was benevolent, the other wrathful.

The Moonchaser True Person’s arm transformed into a wooden spear. Although it was wooden, it was part of the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor. Few materials below the level of immortal treasures could match its strength.

Accompanied by falling petals, the Moon Laurel Spear was tossed forth, piercing through the void and striking the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord squarely in the abdomen. Extending his vajra hand, he effortlessly snapped the Moon Laurel Spear, leaving not a trace of injury on his abdomen.

The Moonchaser True Person mused, An Incipient Dao Fruit for self-reinforcement on the spot? As I thought, he’s a half-immortal. I’m not adept at offensive methods; it looks like I won’t be able to defeat him quickly.

“Two against two! Come, let me witness the abilities of later generations’ cultivators!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s three faces laughed simultaneously, an indescribably eerie sight.

“Who said it was two against two?” The Moonchaser True Person smiled, making the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord feel that something was amiss.

A divine halberd swept horizontally, striking the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord directly in the waist.

“Demon-Subduing, what are you doing?!” the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord bellowed in rage. The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord had suddenly struck him from such close range that he hadn’t even reacted. Fortunately, I was in my Vajra state; otherwise, that blow would have gravely wounded me.

The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord’s eyes were bloodshot as he panted and stared at the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. Only upon hearing the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s Buddhist-toned rebuke did he regain his senses.

“I...I don’t know either! I just found you an eyesore and felt like hitting you,” the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord hastily explained.

“Well? Are you still going to say it’s two against two?” The Moonchaser True Person smirked derisively. When facing a half-immortal born from the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit, having too many allies was the worst idea. By manipulating emotions, the half-immortal could easily sow discord among their ranks.

Even though the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord had also chanted scriptures, he was not a half-immortal, meaning his mental state was inferior to the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. Despite reciting Buddhist texts simultaneously, their resistance to the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit differed.

“It’s just three against one. Do you think I’ll fear you?!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord no longer counted on the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord for assistance. He only prayed the latter wouldn’t cause further trouble.

“Who said it was three against one?”

The ground immediately churned. Thick tree roots burst forth, resembling countless tentacles, yet those tentacles were like white jade and not at all eerie.

Countless roots coiled around the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. He smashed them with his fists, breaking them apart, but the roots only split partially before swiftly regenerating and continuing to entangle him.

Behind the Moonchaser True Person, an identical figure appeared. The two stood side by side, indistinguishable from each other.

“Little Ye, you’re finally awake?”

“I was woken up by the noise,” Bai Ye grumbled.

She simply preferred sleeping in the day; it wasn’t a necessity. Should the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace encounter enemies, she would naturally intervene and resolve the matter.

After all, she was the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace’s trump card at the Transcendent Tribulation Stage.

“The Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor?!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord hadn’t expected the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor to have evolved to such an extent. He had thought it had merely developed consciousness, but now, he realized that it had developed a soul!

“Now, are you still going to say it’s three against one?” The Moonchaser True Person looked down upon him, her gaze icy.

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord felt that perhaps he didn’t need the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor so badly after all.

Chapter 961: Impossible To Guard Against The Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord felt that he had accumulated all his misfortune from eight lifetimes. The battle was overwhelmingly against him. His supposed ally, the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord, was unreliable and could strike him at any moment, and he himself constantly had to recite Buddhist scriptures to regulate his emotions.

On the contrary, the opposing side consisted of a half-immortal, an immortal tree that had developed a soul, and a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator.

Hell, even the Mountain-River Lord himself may not defeat such a lineup, let alone me.

Better to flee.

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord felt the tree roots constricting ever tighter around him.

“Let me save you! I hereby establish the Rule: The Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor cannot regenerate itself !” The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord swung his great halberd with all his might.

Suddenly, a tree root shot out and collided with the great halberd. The halberd severed half the root, but it immediately regenerated.

“How is this possible? I established a rule!”

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord couldn’t hold back his anger. “Fool! The Tree Ancestor has reached the level of an ‘immortal item.’ How could a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator’s rule possibly affect it?!”

“I was kind enough to save you, yet you dare to scold me?!” The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord was similarly enraged. He grabbed his divine halberd and struck the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord on the head, leaving his mind buzzing.

“Let me preface this—I did not use the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit just now,” the Moonchaser True Person declared preemptively.

“Nonsense! That was clearly your doing!” the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord quickly retorted, asserting his stance.

No one could ascertain which side was speaking the truth.

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord had no time to discern truth from falsehood; his priority was to escape.

“Vajra Dharma Manifestation!” With a shout, the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord summoned his Vajra Dharma Manifestation, which was a hundred zhang high. As the roots coiled around the dharma manifestation, he opened a large hole in its chest and fled.

“You think you can come to the immortal palace and just leave?!”

The moment the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord was about to escape the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace’s bounds, countless tree roots surged skyward, rising straight up to form a wooden wall that stretched beyond sight.

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord flew to the right, but the roots were always one step ahead, shooting upward rapidly.

In the blink of an eye, the vast Moon Laurel Immortal Palace was encircled by roots.

The Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor had grown on the Great Snow Mountain since ancient times, its root system extending to every corner of the mountain. Let alone enveloping the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace, even sealing off the entire Great Snow Mountain wouldn’t be difficult.

In theory, the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor was equivalent to a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator, but her sheer size rivaled that of the Epoch Immortal when he was still at the Transcendent Tribulation Stage. She could not be treated as an ordinary Transcendent Tribulation cultivator.

Her only weakness was the inability to leave the Great Snow Mountain’s territory.

“Damn it, do you really think I’m made of clay?!”

Seeing that escape was impossible, the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord flew into a rage. Then I shall fight to the death with the immortal palace. Since they have no particularly powerful offensive methods, my Vajra Incipient Dao Fruit has nothing to fear.

Yet, just as his momentum surged, his ire swiftly dissipated, and his aura plummeted.

He erupted in fury again, only to have it vanish once more.

Again and again.

“Immortal palace, you’ve gone too far!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s eyes bulged, a sense of emptiness gnawing at his heart. It was too unbearable to keep the rage pent up.

The Moonchaser True Person sneered. “Don’t think you can just yell a few times, get angry, and have your power skyrocket. I won’t give you that chance.”

All those storybooks I read weren’t for nothing.

“Demon-Subduing, go kill all the immortal palace disciples!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s brain worked fast despite the emergency. He refused to believe the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace would stand idly by while their disciples were slaughtered.

They’ll surely divert their attention to deal with Demon-Subduing. That will significantly relieve the burden on me.

“Dream on!”

Sect Ancestor Fang produced a wooden box. Upon opening it, a ray of light engulfed the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord just as he was about to unleash a massacre. Unable to resist, he was sucked into the wooden box.

“Light-Retaining Treasure Box.” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord sneered. He had heard of this renowned immortal treasure that was crafted from the horn of a xiezhi. It could suck people in and force them to battle, only allowing departure after a victor was determined.

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy had once challenged four immortal sect leaders inside while controlling Lu Yang’s body, enjoying her moment of glory.

The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord knew his plan had succeeded. Someone will have to enter the treasure box to be Demon-Subduing’s opponent, and that someone must be a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator. Anyone below that will be courting death if they enter.

So that person will be this Transcendent Tribulation surnamed Fang.

To his surprise, Sect Ancestor Fang had anticipated it. She whipped out a bag of ants and stuffed them into the wooden box.

After defeating this army of ants, the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord could only leave. However, the space inside was vast, far beyond what his spiritual consciousness could cover.

“I hope the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord can find all the ants,” Sect Ancestor Fang muttered.

Luo Hongxia’s left eyelid twitched twice. She had never imagined the Light-Retaining Treasure Box could be used in such a way.

No wonder she is the Sect Ancestor.

“Having one fewer opponent is still a good thing!” the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord said through gritted teeth, refusing to admit his scheme had failed. These cultivators from the later generations are damn despicable.

“Vanquishing Devils and Upholding the Dao!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord drew upon the power of incense offerings, transforming into the peerless divine lord who had protected the land by vanquishing evil and upholding the Dao three hundred thousand years ago.

The jade dragon exhaled extremely frigid air, freezing the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord in place. The Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit disrupted his emotions, rendering his attacks and defenses disjointed, while the tree roots flogged him mercilessly.

Boom!

The chunk of ice shattered. The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord emerged slowly, the corners of his mouth curling into a wild, arrogant smile. “Come on! Let’s see how your attacks can harm me!”

“Come on then!” The Moon Laurel Immortal Palace didn’t hold back, launching a second round of assault.

Observing from close range, Lu Yang noticed something amiss. “Is there something off about the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s state? He seems to be rather enjoying this. Could Senior Moonchaser have tampered with him, making him perceive pain as pleasure?”

Lu Yang found the Seven Emotions Incipient Dao Fruit truly terrifying; its abilities were impossible to guard against, and one wouldn’t even know they had been affected.

Fortunately, Senior Moonchaser is on our side.

Lu Yang abruptly looked up, sensing danger. A stray attack was heading toward him and Jinghong.

“Senior Sister, clash palms!” Lu Yang hollered.

Jinghong instantly understood his intention. Their palms collided, propelling each other backward, out of the attack’s range.

“Hmm? Why has someone else infiltrated the immortal palace?” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy frowned.

“Fight, fight; the fiercer, the better.” A figure watched the beleaguered Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord being pummeled and chuckled to himself.

The two divine lords were mere decoys to distract the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace. I am the one who will chop down the Tree Ancestor.

“Hmm?” The figure suddenly felt a burning sensation in his chest. He pulled out a set of four tokens, one of them glowing hot. He cradled the token, trembling with excitement. “This is... One of the Four Ancient Immortals is nearby?!”

In that moment, he had completely tossed the matter of the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor to the back of his mind. Compared to the whereabouts of the Four Ancient Immortals and their Dao Fruits, what is the Tree Ancestor?

He kept moving about, searching for the direction where the token burned hottest. Soon, his gaze settled on the black ring in Jinghong’s hand.

Inside that ring is an ancient immortal!

He struck toward the unsuspecting Jinghong. If she took that hit, death would be inevitable!

The entity within the ancient ring sighed. Now that things have come to this, I have no choice but to intervene.

A vortex appeared in the ancient ring, absorbing the attack inside.

That figure seized the opportunity to enter the ring.

“Specter Lord of the Great Qian Dynasty pays respects to the Heaven Mandate Immortal.”

Chapter 962: Great Venerable Dragon Mandate

“Specter Lord of the Great Qian Dynasty pays respects to the Heaven Mandate Immortal.” The Specter Lord bowed with cupped fists upon entering the ancient ring.

He possessed four tokens in total. Whenever one of the Four Ancient Immortals was nearby, the corresponding token would react. When he entered the small world within the ancient ring, the token inscribed with the characters “Heaven Mandate” became scorching hot.

The entity before me is none other than the legendary Heaven Mandate Immortal, the one who established the ancient order, commanded the power of heavenly tribulations, and was hailed as the progenitor of artifact refinement!

Merely being under the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s gaze sent chills down the Specter Lord’s spine. It’s as if I’m standing before the Emperor-Lord himself.

“You may behold one’s immortal visage.”

With the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s permission, the Specter Lord raised his head.

A golden wall adorned with patterns of fish scales greeted him. As the golden wall shifted, the Specter Lord immediately realized that this was no golden wall. It was the body of a golden true dragon!

He lifted his head even higher, meeting the gaze of the golden dragon.

In the pure white space, a golden dragon coiled, its head lowered slightly as it stared at the Specter Lord with an inscrutable expression.

It was impossible to tell how many thousands of zhang the golden dragon stretched; it was longer than any true dragon that the Specter Lord had seen. Its entire body seemed cast from molten gold, with a pair of wings sprouting from its back, and its head radiated an innate majesty. Even the most skilled artisan couldn’t replicate a tenth of its immortal prestige.

The Specter Lord maintained his smile, though inwardly he was marveling. The legend is true—the Heaven Mandate Immortal is indeed the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate of the East Sea.

The golden dragon snorted white dragon breath. Its heartless eyes stared at the token in the Specter Lord’s hand as a deep voice reverberated through the ancient ring’s small world, leaving the Specter Lord’s ears buzzing.

“It seems your Great Qian Dynasty does have some skill; you could track down my whereabouts.

“Attacking Jinghong to force one’s hand. What is it you seek from me?” There was a note of interrogation in the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate’s voice.

Once he intervened, Lu Yang would likely notice something amiss and discover his presence.

“My master wishes to gather the power of the Four Ancient Immortals to break the seal of the cage and open a passage to the external world. I entreat you to grant this request, Immortal Venerable.”

The Midheaven Emperor-Lord had discovered that the seal of this hexahedral world was set by the Four Ancient Immortals using the power of their Dao Fruits. To undo this prison world’s seal, the Four Ancient Immortals’ aid was required. Hence, he forged four tokens to locate them and plead his case.

If I can rope the Heaven Mandate Immortal in to our Great Qian’s side, that will be a far greater meritorious contribution than chopping down the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor.

Besides, the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor is right here. I can visit anytime. But an encounter with the Heaven Mandate Immortal is a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity.

“Breaking the seal is possible.”

The Specter Lord was overjoyed. He had not expected the legendary Heaven Mandate Immortal to be so amenable. He didn’t even get to use the lengthy script he had prepared. “Then...”

“But not now.”

“May I ask when you will break the seal, Immortal Venerable?”

“When the sixth immortal is slain.”

The Specter Lord didn’t understand who the Heaven Mandate Immortal meant by the “sixth immortal.” Is it the enemy from the battle that refined the stars into continents?

And who is the fifth immortal?

“When will the sixth immortal be found?”

“I know not.”

“You know not?” The Specter Lord was stunned. If the timing isn’t known, how long must we wait? Will the Emperor-Lord have to wait another three hundred thousand years? Do we have to sleep for another three hundred thousand years?

“Setting aside the fact that Everlasting has yet to revive and cannot break the seal, the sixth immortal is an unscrupulous individual. In the past, the destruction of the Ember Legacy Dynasty and the abject misery of living beings were caused by the sixth immortal’s machinations. If the world’s seal is broken and the sixth immortal escapes, further calamity will surely befall the world. Can your Great Qian Dynasty bear responsibility for this?”

“Immortal Venerable, you are benevolent, but what do the common people have to do with us cultivators?” The Specter Lord smiled. If they cared about the common folk, they wouldn’t have allowed the Great Qian Dynasty to fall, plunging the world into chaos and conflict.

“The common people are resilient. When one generation perishes, another is born in its place. Why should you worry about their lives and deaths, Immortal Venerable?

“This matter is of great consequence, and my master regards it seriously. I humbly request that you meet my master in person to discuss it. What say you, Immortal Venerable?”

“And if one refuses?”

“Then I hope you won’t take offense at this junior’s impertinence.

“Immortal Venerable, you are not in the best condition, are you? I heard that the one who died at the Sword Tower was a living being born from your corpse. Bereft of your physical body, how much of your world-shaking power can you use?”

The Specter Lord still wore a smile, though it was no longer an ingratiating smile. It had become more expressive, heavy with mockery.

He had assessed the power of the Heaven Mandate corpse at the Sword Tower. That corpse possessed only the physical body and could barely be called an immortal. It is the weakest existence among immortals.

Even though the Heaven Mandate soul is separated from the physical body, he didn't kill the Heaven Mandate corpse to reclaim his body. This means that the Heaven Mandate Immortal isn't even confident of defeating the Heaven Mandate corpse; their strengths are evenly matched.

If so, I shall proceed with the original plan—kill the Heaven Mandate Immortal and seize the Tribulation Mandate Dao Fruit.

“You're seeking death.”

At the same time, the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate transmitted an auditory message to Jinghong, warning her not to enter the ancient ring until the matter was settled.

“Then I will have to ask you to leave your life here!”

A divine talisman appeared on the Specter Lord’s brow. The talisman rune transformed into an emperor reigning over the nine heavens. It was none other than the Midheaven Emperor-Lord, whose appearance had never been captured in any portrait!

The Midheaven Emperor-Lord opened his eyes, as though he was capable of crushing all under heaven, preventing anyone from entertaining the slightest thought of resistance.

“A mere clone formed from the power of incense offerings. Do you think it can turn the heavens upside down?” The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate sneered. If the true form of the Midheaven Emperor-Lord had come, I would indeed be unable to defeat him in his current state. But to think a mere clone can deal with me? Utter delusion.

The Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone extended a hand and grasped the Specter Lord's head. The latter seemed to have anticipated this from his master’s clone and didn't resist, allowing himself to be absorbed.

The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate raised an eyebrow, spotting something amiss. He felt that the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone was devouring the Specter Lord not just to strengthen the clone. There should be something else.

“Nine Heavens Dark Lightning.”

In an instant, dark clouds covered the sky in the ancient ring’s small world, lightning flashing and thunder rolling. Every bolt of lightning could cause a Fusion cultivator’s soul to disintegrate, and there were millions of such lightning bolts here!

The lightning bolts gathered and fused, forming a crimson dark lightning bolt that locked onto the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone’s karma and struck it down decisively.

To his surprise, a massive black vortex appeared above the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone. Crimson dark lightning descended, only to be devoured by the black vortex without stirring the slightest ripple.

“Devouring? Storing? Transferring?” The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate’s mind raced through various possibilities as he speculated which Dao Fruits the Midheaven Emperor-Lord possessed.

The second dark lightning fell, encompassing terrifying power and boundless light. Once again, it was devoured by the black vortex, as if it had never appeared.

Despite undergoing the cleansing from the lightning tribulation, the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone remained untouched, even strolling through the lightning tribulation with ease.

“Interesting. Immortals of later generations do have some skill.”

Next

Chapter 963: Silent Annihilation Immortal, Don’T Even Think About Deceiving One!

“Earth Calamity.”

With a mere thought, the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate caused three stars to descend from the lightning tribulation, hurtling toward the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone.

These were not true tribulation lightning bolts but stars that had been compressed and transformed through the Tribulation Mandate Dao Fruit. Originally, each star was so enormous that a single one would fill the entire space. After compression, three could strike the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s clone simultaneously with attacks that were astonishingly dense, exceedingly hard, and more devastating than the original stars.

If it were the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord, who was currently locked in fierce combat outside, one such falling star would be all it took to shatter his Vajra body, let alone three.

Numerous black vortices materialized around the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone. The three stars descended and exploded, shaking the entirety of the small world. The black vortices dug a hole straight through the stars, and the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone emerged from the hole, still completely unscathed.

But the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate had now ascertained the nature of the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s Dao Fruit.

“As I thought, it is a devouring-type Dao Fruit.”

Had it been a transferring- or space-type Dao Fruit, it would not have drilled a hole through the stars—it would have made them disappear into thin air. Transferring an entire star would be far easier than transferring only a portion.

“How’s that, Immortal Venerable?” The voice that emerged from the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone was suddenly the Specter Lord’s, dripping with ridicule and derision.

If the Specter Lord hadn’t known that the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s Dao Fruit could devour all manner of heavenly tribulations, he would not have been so confident.

“Trite and unworthy of mention. When Qilin withstood my heavenly tribulations, he grew stronger. Mere devouring is nothing,” the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate commented coldly, unmoved.

“Let’s hope you can still say that in the face of death, Immortal Venerable.”

Fire Calamity, Water Calamity, Wind Calamity—the three calamities with the world-annihilating power descended in succession, interwoven with various lightning tribulations. All were devoured by the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone.

“You dare approach one?” The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate extended a dragon claw toward the clone.

Having lost his physical body, this was a pure soul attack. The Specter Lord hidden within the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone would absolutely be unable to withstand such a blow. Rather than engaging in a contest of Dao Fruits, directly attacking the Specter Lord’s soul was far easier.

Yet, the moment the dragon claw touched the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone, a vortex appeared beside the clone once more and devoured the whole of the claw!

The Devouring Dao Fruit had devoured a soul!

From how the Devouring Dao Fruit had behaved earlier, its devouring speed was certainly not this fast. Some portion of the soul attack should have reached the Specter Lord.

Unless the Devouring Dao Fruit now devours souls faster than it devours matter!

“Immortal Venerable, why not guess why my master’s clone devoured me?” The Specter Lord’s mocking laugh rang out once more from the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone.

During the Great Qian era, the Specter Lord’s divine office was to govern ghosts, ensuring they perished in an orderly manner without affecting the mortal realm. As a result, though his Incipient Dao Fruit belonged to the lineage of the Soul Dao Fruit, it was not simply connected to souls in a general sense—it focused on restraining and consuming them.

After the Devouring Dao Fruit devoured the Specter Lord, its speed at devouring souls had been enhanced.

The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate could no longer attack with his soul!

Dragon roars echoed through the sky as the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate and the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone engaged in an increasingly terrifying contest. The clone fought with growing ferocity, while the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate began to show signs of fatigue, as if on the verge of defeat.

“Immortal Venerable, it seems your abilities aren’t as formidable as the legends would have it.” The Specter Lord laughed heartily, glancing disdainfully at the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate. To overpower an ancient personage and even slay him—there is nothing more satisfying.

“Is that all you have? Nothing special at all!

“Are you too weak, or is my master’s clone too strong?

“The victor becomes the king, and the loser becomes a bandit. I’ll be taking the Tribulation Mandate Dao Fruit!”

Just when the Specter Lord believed the outcome was decided, the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate, who had covered the sky, suddenly contracted inward, condensing into a man who bore the bearing of both an emperor and an ethereal immortal. The two completely different auras coexisted on him without the slightest incongruity.

“So this is your true form, Immortal Venerable? Shrinking to facilitate escape...” The Specter Lord thought nothing of it and continued to jeer. But before he could finish, the Heaven Mandate Immortal instantly teleported to the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s clone with unimaginable speed, seizing him by the throat.

“You...you have a physical form!” The Specter Lord stared at the true Heaven Mandate Immortal in disbelief, completely devoid of his earlier arrogance.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal gazed coldly at the Specter Lord hiding within the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone and said calmly, “Fool. Since you know I am the progenitor of artifact refinement, did it never occur to you that I could forge a body from immortal metal?”

That said, he had only forged a portion of an immortal body from immortal metal; many parts still lacked a physical form.

But against this clone, it’s more than sufficient.

“If not for one’s worry that unleashing stronger heavenly tribulations would shatter this small world and attract Silent Annihilation, how could the mere Devouring Dao Fruit possessed by this clone have devoured one’s heavenly tribulations?

“Remember, this is the tribulation of arrogance and hubris.”

“You...you cannot kill me! Killing me will make you an enemy of the Great Qian Dynasty! My master will not spare you!” The Specter Lord panicked, realizing he had significantly underestimated the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s prowess.

This is not an entity I can scheme against!

“Long-winded.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal’s right hand tightened, and a lightning tribulation condensed to the extreme appeared in his palm.

The Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone was instantly sundered into pieces. The Specter Lord did not even have time to cry out.

“Strange. The third infiltrator had a token inscribed with “Heaven Mandate” and was about to attack that lass Jinghong when he got sucked into the ancient ring.”

A thought struck Lu Yang. “Could there be clues about Senior Heaven Mandate inside that ancient ring? And isn’t there a senior’s soul inside it? Could that senior be in danger?”

“Xiao Yangzi, go and check on the situation.”

Lu Yang hurried to Jinghong’s side. Having heard her shifu’s warning, Jinghong realized that a great battle was unfolding within the ancient ring. She was so terrified that she could barely speak coherently.

After all, unlike Lu Yang, she had never experienced an immortal battle at such close range.

“Immortal Fairy, this...”

“Simple. I’ll pull both of you in.”

Without a second word, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy pulled Lu Yang and Jinghong into the ancient ring’s small world.

The small world within the ring was a scene of utter devastation, clearly having been ravaged by a great battle. Lu Yang and the Everlasting Immortal Fairy looked up at the sky, seeming to have sensed something.

In the sky, the Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone was slowly crumbling to dust in the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s grasp, fading with the wind.

“Heaven Mandate Immortal!” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy pointed at him, her face lighting up with joy. I’ve finally encountered someone alive.

Lu Yang hadn’t expected the Heaven Mandate Immortal to be here. He had thought there would at most be some clues about him.

“Helpers?!” The Heaven Mandate Immortal sensed people barging into the ancient ring and assumed they were reinforcements from the Great Qian Dynasty.

He looked down and saw the Everlasting Immortal Fairy. His heart sank. As I thought, I still attracted the enemy I had hoped to avoid. “Silent Annihilation Immortal!”

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy flew into the air and excitedly waved at the Heaven Mandate Immortal. “What do you mean, 'Silent Annihilation Immortal'? It’s me, Everlasting!”

To her dismay, the Heaven Mandate Immortal refused to believe her, rebuking sternly. “What Everlasting? You’re genuinely looking down on one, Silent Annihilation. You’ve already deceived me once. Do you think I would fall for it again?!”

He had not fought with his full strength earlier, fearing the small world would collapse and attract the Silent Annihilation Immortal. But now that she’s already here, there is no more reason to hold back.

The small world was now filled with the power of heavenly tribulations. Countless heavenly tribulations appeared, terrifying beyond measure.

“Why won’t you believe me? Oh, I know, I’ll self-detonate for you!” Seeing that the Heaven Mandate Immortal refused to believe her, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy had a sudden flash of inspiration. Without hesitation, she prepared to self-detonate as proof.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal’s expression changed drastically. This self-detonation doesn’t look fake!

He hastily withdrew the heavenly tribulation power and called out, “Wait! One—eh, no—I believe—”

BOOM!

Chapter 964: The Later Generations Are Truly Despicable For Spreading Rumors

A terrifying explosion erupted in the sky above the ancient ring’s small world. Despite the deafening boom, under the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s deliberate control, the blast radius was minimal, so small that only the Heaven Mandate Immortal was caught in it, sparing Lu Yang and Jinghong above and below.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal coughed violently, feeling as though his soul was about to be expelled from his immortal body. The Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone had been devouring his soul for quite some time, yet it had not dealt as much damage as this single detonation.

In the blink of an eye, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s soul had fully recovered. She asked eagerly, “Well? Do you believe me now? If not, I can self-detonate a couple more times.”

Hearing those words snapped the dazed Heaven Mandate Immortal back to his senses. He waved his hands frantically, fearing that she would self-detonate again if he responded too slowly. “No, no, no! I believe you, I believe you!”

This is definitely not the Silent Annihilation Immortal. She can’t imitate this self-detonation style even if she wants to.

“You really are Everlasting...” the Heaven Mandate Immortal subconsciously murmured. He then abruptly realized how the Everlasting Immortal Fairy could misinterpret his words and quickly corrected himself. “Everlasting, so this is when you’ve been resurrected.”

Through a temporal passage in ancient times, he had glimpsed a future version of the Everlasting Immortal Fairy, but he never knew when that future would be. So it turns out to be four hundred thousand years later.

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy was also overjoyed to see her long-lost friend. “This immortal has been resurrected for several years now. I’ve been searching for the four of you, but to no avail. Little Ling and Lianyi don’t know where you’ve gone. Finally, I’ve found one of you. Where have all of you been all these years?”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal sighed. “That is a long story.”

“If it’s a long story, save it for later. Come, come, I’ll introduce you to the person who resurrected me.”

Although Jinghong was not harmed by the immortal soul explosion, she had been shaken awake. Coming to her senses, she found herself in the ancient ring's small world, with Lu Yang beside her. Her shifu was in the sky, conversing with a young immortal lady.

Jinghong was bewildered. “Why does Shifu sound different from usual? And why has he transformed into human form?”

“Hasn’t he always been human?” Lu Yang questioned.

“How could Shifu be human? He said he is the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate...” Jinghong began to explain instinctively. “Wait, Junior Brother Lu Yang, you know Shifu?” She suddenly realized from Lu Yang’s calm tone that he seemed to be familiar with her shifu.

Lu Yang. “...”

Heaven Mandate Immortal, so you’ve been appearing to Senior Sister Jinghong only in your Dragon Mandate form?

Lu Yang sighed. “Just a coincidence, I suppose. I’ve heard stories about your shifu’s past and even met him a few times, though not his true self.”

“Shifu’s past? Like how he proved the Dao through strength alone, split the heavens with a single axe, commanded all tribulations, subdued the Epoch Immortal, and suppressed the Qilin Immortal?

“Or how he split mountains with his axe, carving a path toward light for humanity? Or how he set up a chess game with yin and yang, comprehended the Great Dao, transcended a death tribulation, and played against the Qilin Immortal, only to realize a century had passed in the blink of an eye?”

Lu Yang mulled over it. The latter two events likely correspond to the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s impoverished youth, when he had split mountains to block travelers and demand tolls, and the time he played Five in a Row with the Qilin Immortal for a century just to avoid the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s cooking.

“Uh, something like that.”

“Let me tell you. It was Lu Yang who called out my name and brought me back to life. I have great hopes for this young man. He is now the Second-in-Command of our Everlasting Lineage...”

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy descended with the Heaven Mandate Immortal to introduce Lu Yang to him.

“Greetings, Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal,” Lu Yang said hurriedly. Although the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s narrations painted the Heaven Mandate Immortal as an eccentric and unlucky immortal, he is nevertheless a top-tier powerhouse in the world. I mustn’t slight him, especially with how much I know about his embarrassing past. I hope the Heaven Mandate Immortal will requite enmity with kindness and not hold it against a junior like me.

“Hahaha, Young Friend, there’s no need for such formality. You resurrected Everlasting; the four of us immortals couldn’t be more grateful. I should be the one to bow to you.” With that, the Heaven Mandate Immortal cupped his hands and bowed with the most authentic ancient etiquette, saying solemnly, “My gratitude to you, Young Friend Lu Yang.”

“Shifu, who is this?”

“This is Everlasting, whom I told you about. I didn’t expect her to be resurrected in this era.”

“Greetings, Senior Everlasting.”

“This is my disciple, Jinghong; you probably already know her. Although her talent is average, her character is not bad. Under my guidance, she has cleansed her marrow and exchanged her bones, undergoing a complete transformation. She can now be considered one of the top prodigies of this age.

“However, a tall tree attracts the wind. To avoid attracting unnecessary trouble, I advised her to keep a low profile. That’s why she remains unknown to the world.”

“You accept disciples?” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy recalled that the Heaven Mandate Immortal had never taken a disciple before.

“Explaining the whole story would be quite troublesome. Let’s set it aside for now. I’ll first help to deal with the Great Qian cultivators outside.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal hadn’t forgotten about the two divine lords outside.

Since they’re all cultivators from the Ember Legacy Dynasty, they’re likely cut from the same cloth as the Specter Lord. If the Specter Lord is staying here, they should too.

“Are you going to leave the ancient ring’s small world?”

“No need. I have other means.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal smiled proudly.

“Immortal Palace, your attacks don’t even tickle. Is that all you’ve got?” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord cackled out loud to bolster his courage while inwardly cursing the Specter Lord for not making his move yet. How much longer are you going to take? If this drags on, Great Xia cultivators may come to the immortal palace’s aid.

As I thought, not a single one on my side is dependable.

Faced with the combined assault of the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace trio, the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord could only defend passively. However, the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace lacked the means to breach his defenses, and he likewise lacked the means to escape.

The battle had reached an impasse.

Just as the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord was still grumbling about the Specter Lord, an explosion resounded from deep within the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace.

“Success!” The Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord’s heart leaped with joy. I can finally wrap things up.

Wait, the explosion didn’t come from the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor!

A crimson-gold flame erupted from beneath the pill concoction chamber, its brilliance undiminished even under the sunlight.

The flame transformed into a spear, hurtling straight toward the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. A sense of crisis gripped him, and he crossed his arms before his chest. However, the crimson-gold spear ignored all defenses, piercing through his arms and chest.

The spear then softened, morphing into a fiery rope that bound him tightly. The searing flame scorched his body, bones, and soul, causing him to cry out in agony.

“Is this the immortal fire passed down from the ancient era?” The Moonchaser True Person was astonished. No one has ever been able to refine or move this fire. Why has it taken the initiative to strike now?

“Could it have developed feelings for the immortal palace, emerging when we face a life-or-death crisis?”

“Isn’t this the immortal fire I left behind from my barbecuing? You can use it?”

“It’s naturally yours, Everlasting. But since that mastermind erased your existence, many of your possessions became ownerless. This immortal fire is one of them.

“Jinghong needed this wisp of immortal fire for her cultivation, so I secretly refined it.

“Speaking of which, the Mother-Child River is also an ownerless item. Yet somehow, the outside world spread rumors that I created it, that my motives were impure, that I am lecherous, fond of married women, and have impregnated countless women.

“If I ever find out where these rumors originated, I will make them understand what the wrath of an immortal truly means!”

At the mention of that, the Heaven Mandate Immortal couldn’t control his ire. The image I had painstakingly shaped has been instantly destroyed in this era. This grudge is no less than the enmity of obstructing one’s Dao!

“The later generations are truly despicable for spreading rumors!” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy and Lu Yang seethed together with righteous indignation.

Chapter 965: Hui Doudou’S Objective

Finding the culprit behind the rumors would take some time. The Heaven Mandate Immortal set the matter aside for now and prepared to elaborate on what the Everlasting Immortal Fairy did not know.

“Jinghong, step out for a moment. If you were to learn what I am about to share, your life would be in danger.”

“Yes, Shifu.” Jinghong obediently left the ancient ring’s small world.

“And you too, Young Friend Lu Yang...”

“If you are referring to matters concerning the other me, Lu Yang already knows about them.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy saw no need for Lu Yang to leave.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal immediately pieced things together. “That makes sense. You would have to be aware; otherwise, you wouldn’t have been attacked twice by the Silent Annihilation Immortal.

“Since you’ve already met Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, and Young Friend Lu Yang is at the Basic Divine Transfiguration Stage, I presume you have undergone the lightning tribulation and encountered the immortal thought I left behind. You must have learned of the events from your demise to the establishment of the Ember Legacy Dynasty.

“There’s no need for me to repeat those events. I’ll begin from the end of the Ember Legacy Dynasty.

“When we thought we had successfully resurrected you, we came to realize through our interactions that we hadn’t revived the complete you, but the you from the time you possessed the Silent Annihilation Incipient Dao Fruit. Still, being able to revive you was ultimately a good thing, so we didn’t dwell on it. We even told that version of you many things about your life after becoming an immortal.

“That version of you was just like the real you—exceptionally talented with astonishing cultivation speed. She quickly passed the Immortal Ascension Tribulation and attained the Silent Annihilation Dao Fruit, conferring herself the title of ‘Silent Annihilation Immortal Fairy.’

“In resurrecting you, we also harbored the hope of leveraging your power to defeat the mastermind behind the scenes.

“Through our exchanges with the mastermind, we discovered they were immensely powerful. Their combat prowess was no less than yours, having almost reached the pinnacle of the ‘immortal’ realm.

“According to my speculation, the mastermind’s Dao Fruit was most likely the Karma Dao Fruit. That would be extremely troublesome.

“It was precisely this desire to defeat the mastermind that made the situation spiral out of control.

“The Silent Annihilation believed that one must reach the level beyond immortal, ‘Transcendence,’ to defeat the mastermind behind the scenes.”

Lu Yang recalled the Everlasting Immortal Fairy mentioning “Transcendence” and “Omniscience”, the theoretical realms beyond “Immortal” that no one had ever attained.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal continued, “To achieve Transcendence, one must sever all karmic ties. Anyone who knows of her existence would form a karmic bond with her.”

Lu Yang nodded. The Everlasting Immortal Fairy had spoken of similar concepts when we encountered the Second Imperial Preceptor of the Great Yu Dynasty.

“Hence, for the Silent Annihilation Immortal to achieve Transcendence, she needed to kill everyone who knew of her, including all of the Ember Legacy Dynasty and the four of us.

“When the battle first began, the Silent Annihilation Immortal had only recently become an immortal, so her combat power wasn’t impressive yet. But as she killed more and more people, her combat prowess skyrocketed until no one could keep her in check.

“If the four of us, joined by the Left Historian Immortal—who is not a fighter but still an immortal—had fought as one, the five of us could have suppressed the Silent Annihilation Immortal.

“You should have heard from my immortal thought that the Epoch Immortal was assassinated back then. The only one capable of this was the mastermind.”

Lu Yang nodded. Only an existence like the mastermind could have assassinated the Epoch Immortal.

“Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal, I recall your immortal thought saying that after Senior Epoch Immortal perished, the remaining four of you immortals no longer joined hands to oppose the enemy. Why was that?” Even back then, Lu Yang had found it strange, feeling as though something was missing from the narrative.

“My immortal thought was unaware of the intervening events, so its account might have seemed somewhat disjoined.

“At the time, we considered the possibility of an ambush from the mastermind, so we devised an array with the four of us as its core. If any one of us was attacked by an outsider, the other three would be instantly teleported to the scene to fight the enemy together.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal’s voice grew lower. “Yet when the Epoch Immortal was attacked, the array did not activate.

“This meant that the one who ambushed the Epoch Immortal was none other than one of us three!

“With the mastermind’s methods, it was completely possible that they had taken over one of our bodies before the Epoch Immortal’s incident!”

Both Lu Yang and the Everlasting Immortal Fairy started at this revelation.

“If we had chosen to join forces against the Silent Annihilation Immortal back then, we would certainly have been ambushed by the mastermind. None of us would have survived!

“Upon realizing this problem, Qilin, Nine-Layers, and I ceased trusting one another. We even stopped meeting, fighting our own battles separately.

“Meanwhile, the Silent Annihilation Immortal constantly searched for our whereabouts, seeking to achieve Transcendence. Perhaps after she kills me, Qilin, Nine-Layers, and Ao Ling, leaving no one who knows of her existence, that will be the moment she succeeds.

“The Silent Annihilation Immortal seems able to locate those who know of her existence through karmic ties. Later, we discovered that as long as we hid within ‘small world’ spaces, she wouldn’t be able to find us.

“I remember Ao Ling hid within the Dragon Pearl, a small-world-level space. The moment she left the Dragon Pearl, the Silent Annihilation Immortal came for her.”

Lu Yang’s Qingfeng Sword also contained a small-world-level space. Most small-world-level spaces were fixed in place and immovable; these were top-tier secret domains. Items like the ancient ring, the Dragon Pearl, and the Qingfeng Sword were all considered immortal artifacts and exceedingly rare.

“Hiding within a small world requires not only concealing one’s body but also refraining from extending one’s spiritual consciousness outward, lest the Silent Annihilation Immortal detect it.

“After I hid in a secret domain, I could only learn of the outside world through cultivators who entered. However, secret domains didn’t open often, and the information from those cultivators was never comprehensive.

“Therefore, I forged this ancient ring containing a small world, allowing cultivators who entered the secret domain to pick it up. In exchange for guiding their cultivation, they would recount everything they had seen and heard to me.

“From one such cultivator, I learned that a Nascent Soul cultivator had appeared in the outside world—extraordinary combat power, a nascent soul that fought independently, and a wooden plaque on his chest. It sounded very much like a cultivator you had trained, Everlasting.

“Moreover, through the temporal passage before, though I couldn’t see the cultivator standing beside you, I sensed a Jianmu wooden token on his chest.

“I thought you had already been resurrected and went over to investigate.

“It was only upon arriving that I discovered it was a trap set by the Silent Annihilation Immortal!

“At that time, the Silent Annihilation Immortal had grown incomparably powerful. I was no match for her. After a fierce battle, I managed to escape, gravely wounded.

“However, my injuries were too severe. The Dao energy of the Silent Annihilation Dao Fruit ravaged within me. To survive, I desperately performed the Corpse-Liberation Transcendence Method.

“What succeeded was that though I was still critically wounded, I had eradicated the Dao energy, and my life was no longer in danger. What failed was that I lost my physical body—it developed spirit intelligence and sought to devour me.

“I had no choice but to flee, hiding within the ancient ring’s small world to heal while searching for suitable cultivators to continue informing me of affairs in the outside world.

“Three hundred thousand years passed before this child Jinghong obtained the ancient ring.”

Chapter 966: I Also Told Him Many Interesting Stories From Ancient Times

“The previous cultivator who possessed the ancient ring perished within a secret domain that only allowed entry to Foundation Establishment cultivators. At the time, Jinghong was only at the Basic Foundation Establishment Stage, yet she fully displayed her capabilities. Through a twist of fate, she acquired me.

“I observed that this child Jinghong had a good character and showed great potential. With some guidance, she might one day advance to the Transcendent Tribulation Stage or become a half-immortal. If she could live longer, I wouldn’t have to switch between cultivators so frequently.

“Thus, I revealed my identity and expressed that I would like to take her as my disciple. Regardless of whether Jinghong was willing, she naturally wouldn’t miss such a fortuitous opportunity and took me as her shifu.”

Lu Yang remarked inwardly, When you say “revealed my identity,” do you mean your identity as the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate?

Of course, he could only keep these thoughts to himself; he would never dare to voice them aloud.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal continued, “Afterward, I used immortal power to cleanse Jinghong’s marrow, enhance her aptitude, and transform her spirit root, and imparted to her immortal arts and artifact-refinement techniques. When she went out on missions, I even helped her to analyze the situation, guide her thinking, and let her find the answers herself. As I was worried she would attract the Silent Annihilation Immortal’s attention, I urged her to remain as low-key as possible and avoid stirring up trouble.”

Lu Yang. “...”

Everyone else has an immortal assisting them, but why is the difference so vast?

At the mention of this topic, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy perked up. “Oh, we’re quite similar then! After Lu Yang resurrected me, to repay the favor, I helped him...”

She suddenly faltered. Xiao Yangzi’s aptitude is top-tier and doesn’t need enhancement. He also didn’t learn immortal arts from me, and I don’t know artifact-refinement techniques. When he went on missions, I mostly just watched the spectacle to let him develop independent thinking.

“To stir up trouble,” Lu Yang offered helpfully.

“Oh, right, I helped him to stir up trouble...Hmm?”

Seeing that the Everlasting Immortal Fairy was catching on, Lu Yang quickly added, “Immortal Fairy, you also taught me the Play-Dead Technique.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal nodded. “The technique of deceiving heaven and defying fate? It seems Everlasting really values you.”

“Are you describing the same technique?”

“Of course, it’s the same technique. It’s just that Everlasting prefers simpler names, while I like to add a bit of embellishment.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal noticed he was straying from the topic and steered it back. “Young Friend Lu Yang, you have a distinctive way of doing things, you’re full of youthful spirit. I’ve heard many stories about you from Jinghong. What first drew my attention was the time you were attacked by the Silent Annihilation Immortal in the East Sea.

“Not only did you survive that incident, but you later encountered the Silent Annihilation Immortal again at the Sword Tower and survived once more, completely unscathed.

“To survive both encounters unharmed—even I’m not that capable. This made me suspect that you were a bait arranged by the Silent Annihilation Immortal. Although I heard that you underwent the Qian-Heaven Lightning Tribulation when advancing to the Divine Transfiguration Stage, that could have been fabricated by the Silent Annihilation Immortal. I couldn’t be sure if you were truly the person I was seeking.

“Hence, after you arrived at the immortal palace, I instructed Jinghong to keep her distance from you. Judging by your behavior at the immortal palace, I even suspected that you had formed the Harem Golden Core. I intended to observe further to ascertain your identity, but then this incident occurred, and Everlasting brought you directly into the ancient ring’s small world, saving me the trouble.”

“What’s the matter with this body of yours?” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy noticed that the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s body was not physical flesh but an incomplete body forged from immortal metal. Only his arms and legs were made of immortal metal; the rest were crafted from ordinary materials.

“Since I lost my original body, I wasn’t worried about my corpse coming after me. I could easily handle that fool. It’s just that I couldn’t locate him.

“What truly concerned me was the Silent Annihilation finding me. Even at my peak, I couldn’t defeat her, let alone now, when all that remains is my soul.

“To protect myself, I forged this body. But with my limited mobility, I couldn’t find sufficient immortal metal, so I made do with other materials for now. If I could forge an entire body from immortal metal, I would more or less return to my peak state.”

“Speaking of immortal metal... After we eliminated your corpse, quite a lot of immortal metal remained, though Huidoudou mixed them all together, making them inseparable.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal’s eyes lit up. “That’s not a problem. By refining them slowly, I believe I’ll eventually separate that mass of immortal gold.”

To deal with me, my corpse must have gathered vast quantities of immortal metal, enough for me to forge a complete body.

Indeed, corpses are most useful when they’re dead.

“By the way, do you know anything about the Heavenly Court Creed? I’ve heard that even Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi have joined it. Could it be that the Heavenly Court Creed was established by the Silent Annihilation Immortal? But that doesn’t make sense; if she established it, how could Jiang Lianyi still join?

“Or could the Silent Annihilation Immortal have disguised herself as you, Everlasting, and tricked Jiang Lianyi into joining?

“It doesn’t seem like something Qilin or Nine-Layers would create. Such ostentatious actions would attract the Silent Annihilation Immortal’s attention.”

When the Heaven Mandate Immortal first heard of the ancient Heavenly Court that emerged out of nowhere, his heart had tightened sharply. No matter how I think about it, the ancient Heavenly Court sounds like something the Silent Annihilation Immortal would cook up.

“Actually, I was the one who fabricated the Heavenly Court.” Lu Yang scratched his head sheepishly. I didn’t expect the ruse I had devised to deceive the Nine-Netherworlds Creed to grow so large.

“You fabricated it?” The Heaven Mandate Immortal was flabbergasted. He had regarded the ancient Heavenly Court as a formidable foe all this time, never expecting that it was the work of allies.

“Then who is Dou Heavenly Sovereign?”

“That’s me, of course. After all, I’m called Huang Doudou,” the Everlasting Immortal Fairy declared proudly, pleased with her title.

“So your real name is Huang Doudou, Everlasting.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal didn’t know whether to laugh or cry. Only then did he realize that in his elation over the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s resurrection, he had forgotten to ask her name.

He was Si Qi; the Qilin Immortal was Shihe; the Nine-Layers Immortal was Yi Nong; and the Epoch Immortal was Sui Qi. Her name is really incongruous with ours.

“However, the name ‘Heavenly Court’ couldn’t have been conceived out of nowhere, right? Young Friend Lu Yang must have heard it somewhere.” It would be too much of a coincidence.

“Naturally, it was this immortal who told him about the four of us fabricating tales regarding the Heavenly Court and how each of us concocted different versions to fool later generations.”

“So that’s how it is.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal nodded. Now that makes sense.

“It wasn’t just the Heavenly Court. Whenever I was bored, I also told him many interesting stories from ancient times, like how the four of you would eat my cooking and immediately fall asleep, how you often lectured cultivators about maintaining prestige, and that immortal sword you forged for the Qilin Immortal that would soften at the slightest contact... Oh, right! Lu Yang even found that book, Immortals’ Quotes. That was such a good book; I thought it was lost forever...”

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy recounted fervently, not noticing how the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s expression shifted with every anecdote she mentioned.

But Lu Yang noticed.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal was originally all smiles, but now, his expression was as dark as stormy clouds.

His eyes creased with a smile as he patted Lu Yang’s shoulder. “I observe that Young Friend Lu Yang’s cultivation still lacks some refinement. As a cultivator, the most important thing is to cultivate. Young Friend, you must advance to the Void Refinement Stage as soon as possible.

“Rest assured, Young Friend. With me here, when you advance to the Void Refinement Stage and undergo the lightning tribulation, you will definitely emerge unscathed.”

Chapter 967: Immortal Fairy, Let’S Not Be Fated In The Next Life

Although the Heaven Mandate Immortal was smiling, Lu Yang could not feel any warmth from it.

Lu Yang swallowed hard. In the end, there’s still no escaping this ordeal.

Besides, if I remember correctly, the Immortals’ Quotes found in the Twins River space contained not only the words of the Heaven Mandate Immortal but all four ancient immortals...

Is this the Immortal Fairy’s test? It's too difficult.

My fate profile isn’t tough enough. My life probably ends here.

Immortal Fairy, if we are fated, let’s meet again in the next life.

“There’s no need to give him preferential treatment.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy cared for Lu Yang. The latter was deeply moved, feeling as though he had found a lifeline.

She continued, “Didn’t you say that the greater the lightning tribulation’s power, the better the subsequent payoff for the cultivator? Just give him the hardest version.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal clasped his hands behind his back and nodded with a smile. “Of course, of course.”

“...” Lu Yang was silent. Immortal Fairy, let’s not be fated in the next life. I’m afraid I’ll meet my end at your hands again.

“Do you plan to continue staying in this small world?”

“I’ll stay here until this child Jinghong completes her tutelage under me.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal believed in seeing things through.

“Do you want to head to the Dao-Seeking Sect with us? I reckon Lass Yun would like to meet you.”

“Who is Lass Yun?”

“Lu Yang’s Big Senior Sister.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal pondered for a bit and saw no harm in a visit. “Sure.

“Oh, by the way, during the fight with that so-called Specter Lord from Great Qian, he left behind these four tokens.” He produced four tokens from behind his back.

They were remarkably sturdy. The Midheaven Emperor-Lord clone had been obliterated in the lightning tribulation, yet these tokens survived. Their origins were clearly extraordinary.

The token inscribed with “Heaven Mandate” grew so hot in the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s hand that it became scorching. However, with his immortal metal arm, the temperature was no different from cool water.

The other three tokens were respectively inscribed with “Epoch,” “Qilin,” and “Nine-Layers.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal analyzed. “He likely used this token to locate me. The closer it is to me, the hotter it becomes. I haven’t figured out the principle behind it yet.

“The other three tokens should function similarly. Keeping them here would be of little use. It’s better to have Young Friend Lu Yang keep it.”

Lu Yang accepted the three tokens.

Seeing that the conversation had come to a close, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy led Lu Yang out of the ancient ring’s small world.

Jinghong had been waiting outside. She was curious about what her shifu and the others were discussing, but dared not pry. When Lu Yang finally emerged, she asked in puzzlement. “Junior Brother Lu Yang, you’re finally out. Where is that very pretty female immortal?”

Lu Yang tapped his temple. “In my head. Anyway, what’s the current state of the battle?”

“With my shifu employing the immortal fire, he quickly captured the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord has yet to emerge from the Light-Retaining Treasure Box.”

Lu Yang nodded. At this point, the outcome is essentially decided. There’s also nothing to fear about further unforeseen circumstances. After all, we have two immortals on our side.

After a while, Master Jiesha of the Hanging Monastery and Shangguan Yu of the Five-Elements Sect rushed over to aid the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace after receiving the news. They joined forces to defeat the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord.

“They dare come to our immortal palace with this level of skill?” The Moonchaser True Person remarked smugly after their decisive victory.

“Damn it, I knew the Specter Lord was unreliable!” The two divine lords could no longer hold back their anger. Relying on the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s favor, the Specter Lord had been in charge of the operation, using the two of them as bait while he intended to take credit for felling the tree.

And after all that fighting, there is still no sign of him.

“Look on the bright side. Maybe the Specter Lord encountered an immortal and is already dead,” the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord speculated maliciously.

“Greetings, Senior.” Shangguan Yu bowed to the Moonchaser True Person and Bai Ye.

Master Jiesha was still practicing the Mouth-Shut Chan Meditation, so he simply cupped his hands as a gesture.

“What senior? I just have some history with the immortal palace. I happened to pass by and saw they were in trouble, so I lent a hand.” The Moonchaser True Person wasn’t about to admit her true identity.

“If you say so, Senior,” Shangguan Yu replied with a smile. She was happy to converse with the Moonchaser True Person; it made her feel young.

“Hey, young one!”

Lan Ting and Tao Yaoye also rushed over.

“Senior Brother, you’re here. When we came by earlier, you weren’t.”

During the battle, they had searched everywhere for Lu Yang, but since he was inside the ancient ring’s small world, they naturally could not find him.

“Junior Sister Lan, Junior Sister Tao, are you both alright?”

“Yes, yes, we’re fine.” The two blushed as they met Lu Yang’s eyes, unavoidably recalling the “great battle” in Lu Yang’s guest room.

“Junior sisters, if you are confused about what happened earlier, there’s no need. From what I understand, an Incipient Dao Fruit spiraled out of control, setting off the chain of events.”

“Yes, yes, yes! It was all the fault of that Incipient Dao Fruit!” Regardless of whether it was the true reason, the two hastily went along with Lu Yang first and shifted the blame onto the Moonchaser True Person.

As for the truth, it is not important.

Then Bai Ye approached Lu Yang as well. “Apologies, Senior Brother Lu Yang. The immortal palace needs me right now. After I’ve dealt with the Great Qian cultivators, I’ll go find you at the Dao-Seeking Sect to play.”

“Sure, I’ll be waiting for you at the Dao-Seeking Sect.” Lu Yang smiled. Of all the people I’ve interacted with in the immortal palace, communicating with Bai Ye is the least taxing.

The Moon Laurel Immortal Palace had experienced a great battle. Fortunately, when it began, most of the disciples had been chasing Lu Yang’s wooden clone far away from the battlefield and were not affected.

Only the buildings near the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace’s entrance had been damaged in the battle. These structures were at least ten thousand years old, some dating back to the immortal palace’s founding.

“It’s fine as long as no one was hurt,” the Moonchaser True Person said, signaling that the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace should not fret over such matters.

“Come to think of it, it’s the Mid-Autumn Festival today.” Spotting a plate of mooncakes on the table, the Moonchaser True Person suddenly recalled the occasion. With her longevity, she often lost track of time.

“A’Ri, I shall not use this clone today, then,” Bai Ye said. She simply preferred this form; it wasn’t her only one. If she didn’t use it, there would be no switching between yin and yang and no change in identity.

With the battle concluded, the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace was filled with jubilation. Lu Yang and Tao Yaoye, who had planned to depart immediately, decided to stay for one more night before leaving.

“Senior Brother, have a mooncake.” Lan Ting grinned as she popped one into his mouth. “Don’t worry. I’ve tested it; no poison this time. You won’t enlarge or shrink.”

Lu Yang took a bite. The Osmanthus-flower filling was indeed refreshing and unique. “Junior Sister Tao, why don’t you have one too?” He offered Tao Yaoye a mooncake as well.

Snow drifted down in thick, swirling flakes. The three lay on the white ground, gazing at the bright moon above, basking in the quiet serenity.

The next day, Lu Yang and Tao Yaoye set out, each carrying a bundle packed by Lan Ting. They contained Osmanthus mooncakes and spirit fruits unique to the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace.

“Senior Brother Lu, Junior Sister Tao, come visit our immortal palace again when you have time.” Lan Ting waved them off.

“Definitely.” Lu Yang turned back and answered loudly with a wave.

Neither Lan Ting nor Tao Yaoye noticed the ancient black ring on Lu Yang’s middle finger.
