Chapter 968: Senior Brother Dai, I’Ve Also Brought Back A Reclusive Expert

At the summit of Heaven’s Gate Peak...

“Junior Brother Dai...I observe that your complexion has improved considerably compared to previous years. Have you grown more adept at managing the Great Mission Hall?”

“Being adept is one thing, but the most important factor is that the three great diabolic creeds have quieted down significantly. I no longer have to waste so much effort in analyzing intelligence regarding their matters. Speaking of which, I really must thank Junior Brother Lu for that.”

Dai Bufan took a sip of Wudao Enlightenment Tea and reclined on the monarch chair, looking comfortable.

If this had been in the past, he wouldn't have had such leisure to visit Heaven’s Gate Peak to sip tea and chat with Yun Zhi. He would have been hunched over his desk, organizing intelligence and issuing missions.

“Little Junior Brother has a distinctive way of doing things, but it often yields unexpected gains. You should have some insight into that, Junior Brother Dai.”

“I wonder what kind of luck Junior Brother Lu has. Wherever he goes, something always happens. I just heard that a battle broke out at the immortal palace yesterday. They captured two Great Qian cultivators—one was a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator and the other a half-immortal.”

Dai Bufan finished the cup of Wudao Enlightenment Tea, then poured himself another.

Back when he first heard Lu Yang report on Transcendent Tribulation and half-immortal battles breaking out here or there, he would be startled. But now, his Dao heart had been tempered through numerous trials. Battles involving Transcendent Tribulation cultivators and half-immortals could no longer stir so much as a ripple.

“It seems the Great Qian cultivators have finally grown restless.”

“Big Senior Sister, I’m back.” Lu Yang waved to Yun Zhi, delight overflowing from his visage.

“Senior Brother Dai, you’re here too.” Lu Yang was surprised to see Dai Bufan. It was rare for the latter to visit Heaven’s Gate Peak.

“Back from the immortal palace?” Yun Zhi signaled with her eyes for Lu Yang to sit.

Just two days prior, she had traveled to the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace to feed a bowl of Twins River water to Elder Zhu Hong. She hadn’t expected that barely two days later, the immortal palace would come under attack by Great Qian cultivators. Fortunately, it didn’t result in any casualties.

“Big Senior Sister, Senior Brother Dai, come try these mooncakes and spirit fruits. They’re specialties of the immortal palace.” Lu Yang retrieved two bundles from his identity jade tablet—one filled with osmanthus mooncakes and the other with spirit fruits he had found delicious after shrinking.

“How thoughtful of you, Little Junior Brother.” Yun Zhi picked up a spirit fruit and took a small bite.

She had numerous dealings with the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace and had tasted such mooncakes and spirit fruits many times before. But since this is a gesture from Little Junior Brother, it holds a different meaning.

Dai Bufan took a mooncake and ate it alongside his Wudao Enlightenment Tea. “Osmanthus-filled mooncakes? It’s been a while since I ate them. That’s very thoughtful of you, Junior Brother Lu.”

“Junior Brother Lu, did you gain anything from your trip to the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace?”

Lu Yang thought, Senior Brother Dai truly has incredible foresight. My trip to the immortal palace has been worthwhile, and I’ve gained much.

“Just yesterday, the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord of the Great Qian Dynasty slipped into the immortal palace with the intention of felling the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor. The Demon-Subduing Divine Lord was at the Transcendent Tribulation Stage, and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord was a half-immortal. However, with Sect Ancestor Fang, the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor, and the Moonchaser True Person from the immortal palace joining forces to fend off the enemy, the immortal palace suffered little damage.”

Dai Bufan showed little surprise, merely letting out an impassive “oh.” “So the half-immortal on the immortal palace’s side was indeed the Moonchaser True Person?” The intelligence he received had only stated that “suspected to be the Moonchaser True Person.” Now, he had directly confirmed the identity through Lu Yang.

“By chance, I learned some of the truth.

“The Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor has developed spirit intelligence and become a true living being, and her name is Bai Ye. Something went wrong during the Moonchaser True Person’s cultivation, and she has no choice but to share a moon laurel avatar with Bai Ye. In the day, the Moonchaser True Person controls it; at night, Bai Ye takes over.

“Bai Ye enjoys listening to my stories. During my time at the immortal palace, she came to me every night. Before long, we became acquainted.”

“So that’s how it is.” Dai Bufan nodded pensively. Junior Brother Lu really is fated with high-level cultivators. He took another bite of his mooncake.

Lu Yang continued, “Since Bai Ye and the Moonchaser True Person cannot leave the Great Snow Mountain, Bai Ye proposed moving one of its peaks to our Dao-Seeking Sect. That way, she and the Moonchaser True Person could come together. From what she said, it sounds like she plans to stay for the long term.”

Cough, cough, cough! Dai Bufan choked on his mooncake, coughing violently and pounding his chest.

“Senior Brother Dai, are you alright?” Lu Yang asked with concern.

Dai Bufan swallowed the mooncake with difficulty and waved to indicate he was fine, then turned to Lu Yang with a peculiar expression. “Are you saying that you’ve abducted the Moon Laurel Immortal Ancestor and the founder of the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace to our Dao-Seeking Sect?”

Lu Yang hastily denied any involvement. “Bai Ye proposed it on her own. It has nothing to do with me.”

“It must have something to do with you.” Dai Bufan knew well that he couldn’t completely believe Lu Yang’s nonsense.

Yun Zhi was also surprised to hear that Bai Ye and the Moonchaser True Person would come to the Dao-Seeking Sect.

“Is there anything else?” Dai Bufan asked, exasperated. I thought I was mentally prepared, yet Lu Yang’s actions still caught me off guard.

“Oh, oh, and one more thing. I encountered a reclusive expert with unfathomable cultivation at the immortal palace. I invited him back. Would you like to meet him, Senior Brother Dai?”

“A reclusive expert?” Dai Bufan was perplexed. “Where?”

Lu Yang showed him the ancient black ring on his middle finger. “Inside this ancient ring, which contains its own small world.”

“In that case, I’ll have to trouble you to introduce him to me, Junior Brother Lu.” Learning about a reclusive expert was part of gathering intelligence.

Yun Zhi raised an eyebrow, instinctively sensing that the “reclusive expert” whom Lu Yang mentioned was no ordinary figure. She followed Lu Yang and Dai Bufan into the ancient ring’s small world.

Inside, mist permeated the air, limiting visibility. Even when Dai Bufan extended his spiritual consciousness, it was useless. The mist of unknown origin was somehow capable of blocking spiritual consciousness.

A massive silhouette flickered in the distance, gone before he could make out what kind of living being it was. Dai Bufan felt inexplicably tense.

“Junior Brother Lu, where is this reclusive expert?” Dai Bufan whispered.

Lu Yang didn’t speak, simply pointing upward.

Dai Bufan looked up and saw that the colossal figure hidden in the mist had drawn near, appearing enormous and elongated.

A giant dragon’s head that seemed to be cast from pure gold emerged from the mist, mysterious and majestic. The dragon lowered its head, its crystalline eyes reflecting Dai Bufan’s figure.

Dai Bufan shuddered. The dragon did not deliberately release its oppressive pressure, but he still sensed a terrifying oppressive pressure emanating from it. This was the unmistakable aura of an individual who had long reigned at the top.

This reclusive expert is undoubtedly a renowned big shot.

“Junior Brother Lu, who exactly is this great expert?”

“This is the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate, worshipped by the East Sea Dragon Tribe. He also has another identity—the Heaven Mandate Immortal, one of the Four Ancient Immortals, who commands the power of heavenly tribulations. With Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal here, you will surely be safe when undergoing tribulations in the future.”

The golden dragon spoke in human tongue, his words sacred and ancient, carrying the desolate essence of primordial ages. “Young one of later generations, you wished to meet one?”

Dai Bufan’s eyes rolled back, and he collapsed to the ground with a thud.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal scratched his scales with a claw, vexed. “Why are cultivators of later generations so timid?”

Chapter 969: Young One Of Later Generations, Dare You Pit Yourself Against One?

“Little Dai still needs to work on his courage.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy popped out. Although Dai Bufan’s cultivation surpasses Xiao Yangzi’s, his courage lags far behind.

“So this is the Heaven Mandate Immortal?” Yun Zhi studied the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate curiously. It seems that Little Junior Brother’s trip to the immortal palace has yielded significant gains; he even found the Heaven Mandate Immortal, who has been missing for three hundred thousand years.

“Mmm, the Immortal Fairy and I happened to run into Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal at the immortal palace.”

The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate descended, mist curling from his mouth. His draconic eyes reflected Yun Zhi’s expressionless visage.

“Young one, are you the Big Senior Sister whom Young Friend Lu Yang spoke of? Everlasting also holds you in high regard and wishes that one could meet you.”

“It is merely undue praise from Senior Immortal Fairy.”

“Praise or not, one will find out with a test. Young one of later generations, dare you pit yourself against one?”

Yun Zhi cupped her hands in an ancient salute. “Then I shall ask that you offer me your guidance, Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal.”

This was not part of the plan. Lu Yang quickly stepped forward to intervene. “Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal, this isn’t really appropriate, is it?”

The Great Venerable Dragon Mandate laughed heartily. “Young Friend Lu Yang, rest assured. One never bullies the weak. Since you’re worried about your Big Senior Sister, one shall let her have three moves.”

“That’s not what I meant...”

“Alright, that’s enough. An immortal’s words cannot be changed. This matter shall be settled as such!”

“Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal, you are most magnanimous,” Yun Zhi said sincerely. In all her battles, she had never encountered anyone who dared grant her three moves; the Heaven Mandate Immortal was the first.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal also had a penchant for combat. His Dragon Mandate form coiled overhead, resembling the legendary supreme immortal king. “Young one of later generations, strike as you will! Let one see what abilities you possess!”

Nearby, the Everlasting Immortal Fairy suddenly recalled something crucial and called out loudly, “By the way, Heaven Mandate, in the East Sea, it was Lass Yun who drove off the other me!”

“Ah? Wasn’t it this fellow Daoist...”

“Falling Cloud Palm.”

A cluster of spotless, white, auspicious clouds appeared in the skies above the small world, coalescing into a palm that descended. The palm was so massive that it stretched beyond sight, dwarfing even the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate.

As the cloud palm fell, it shattered layers of space and slammed the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate directly onto the ground.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal felt as though this palm encompassed countless worlds, its weight astonishing. Moreover, it was an attack that only targeted the soul; he could not even evade it!

Dai Bufan woke up groggy, finding his mind in a jumble. He had somewhat forgotten how he regained consciousness.

“Didn’t I just meet the legendary Heaven Mandate Immortal? He even said he would visit the sect.”

Dai Bufan remembered. I had seen a golden immortal dragon that was ineffably noble. That was the Heaven Mandate Immortal, the subject of numerous legends, hailed as the immortal among immortals and the Progenitor of Artifact Refinement.

“Where is the Heaven Mandate Immortal?”

As he struggled to prop himself up, he saw Yun Zhi slap the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate to the ground. Dust billowed, and through it, he vaguely glimpsed the colossal dragon writhing in pain.

“On second thought, I’ll sleep a little longer.”

Dai Bufan figured he was still dreaming and passed out again.

“Supreme Simplicity Without Form.”

Another palm struck, compressing the Great Venerable Dragon Mandate back into his human form. The Heaven Mandate Immortal felt his immortal metal limbs trembling uncontrollably.

“Supreme Beginning Without Substance.

“Heaven-Shrouding Immortal Lightning! Immovable Immortal Lightning! Golden Palace Immortal Lightning!” The Heaven Mandate Immortal summoned supreme immortal lightning, weaving them into layers of barriers before him. Multiple immortal lightning bolts intermingled and transformed, coalescing into an immortal shield of lightning.

Then, with a boom, he was sent flying by Yun Zhi’s palm. He hurtled backward until he plunged headfirst into the small world’s barrier.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal pushed against the barrier and pulled his head out with a pop. He felt his head buzzing.

First, Everlasting’s self-detonation had dealt me a harsh blow. Now, Yun Zhi has sent me flying in three moves. Why am I getting beaten two days in a row, and by those on my side at that?

In the ancient era, whenever Everlasting was around, my attempts to show off and appear impressive would inexplicably fail. Why is it still the case now?

The Heaven Mandate Immortal strode over with effortless grace. “Hahaha, Fellow Daoist Yun, you truly possess skills. But we immortals seek freedom and ease. Persistently contending in strength and valor would lower our standing. Since we meet as friends through the Dao, let’s stop where appropriate. There's no need to vie for a clear victory, right?”

“Fellow Daoist Heaven Mandate’s words make sense.” Yun Zhi glanced at Dai Bufan, who was sleeping soundly on the ground. “Strange. I thought I saw Junior Brother Dai wake up during the fight just now.”

Seeing that Dai Bufan wouldn’t wake up anytime soon, she formed a seal and sent him floating out of the ancient ring’s small world and back to the Great Mission Hall. Only after that did she begin to discuss serious matters.

“Senior Immortal Fairy, Little Junior Brother, how did you come across Fellow Daoist Heaven Mandate?”

“Fellow Daoist Yun, no need to rush. Let us sit and speak slowly.”

With a thought from the Heaven Mandate Immortal, the ground transformed, sprouting four chairs connected to the earth. As the ancient ring’s small world was his creation, he could reshape it at will.

“Yesterday, aside from the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord, who served as decoys, another person infiltrated the immortal palace...”

After listening to the full story, Yun Zhi ruminated and remarked, “So that’s how it is. The Midheaven Emperor-Lord seeks to open the world. His clone possessed the ability to devour, which suggests that he wields the Devouring Dao Fruit.

“Generally, one cannot use a Dao Fruit without profound comprehension of it. That is why the Replacement Incipient Dao Fruit and Imprint Dao Fruit we obtained have been set aside unused.

“Judging by the Specter Lord’s actions, he planned to seize the Tribulation Mandate Dao Fruit directly. This means that Great Qian has a way to use the Tribulation Mandate Dao Fruit.

“The most likely possibility is that the Devouring Dao Fruit can ignore comprehension limitations, devour other Dao Fruits, and use them as its own. Presumably, it’s because the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s soul cannot control multiple Dao Fruits that he requires the Moon Laurel Tree Ancestor to strengthen his immortal soul.”

“That’s what I thought too.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal and the Everlasting Immortal Fairy both nodded.

Lu Yang mulled over it. He pretended to understand and nodded along.

Yun Zhi continued, “As for the origin and use of these three immortal-searching tokens, the Specter Lord is dead, but the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord may know something.

“I will find an opportunity to gather intelligence at the immortal palace. This should not be difficult.”

It wasn’t easy to capture these two major figures of the Great Qian Dynasty. Both the immortal sects and the imperial court would take this matter seriously. The Moon Laurel Immortal Palace might even resort to a soul search to extract all the information.

“As for one of the four immortals having their body taken over by the mastermind, that is a problem. The mastermind lurks in the shadows. Now that the mastermind has taken over a body, they have partly moved from the shadows to the open. This is an opportunity.

“Little Junior Brother, should you encounter the Qilin Immortal and the Nine-Layer Immortal in the future, you must be extra careful.”

”Understood. But Big Senior Sister, are you so certain that I will encounter the Qilin Immortal and the Nine-Layers Immortal?” Lu Yang recalled that Yun Zhi had always instructed him to stay away from immortal conflicts. Why has her stance shifted?

Lu Yang only received a complex gaze from Yun Zhi in return. He wisely refrained from asking further.

It does seem like I can’t stay away even if I want to.

Previous

Next

Chapter 970: I Can Forge Any Immortal Artifact

“Fellow Daoist Heaven Mandate, I observe that your body is incomplete. Would you require some immortal metal to restore it?”

Without the Heaven Mandate Immortal needing to voice it, Yun Zhi discerned his condition and what he sought.

“In that case, thank you, Fellow Daoist Yun Zhi!” The Heaven Mandate Immortal was overjoyed. This was exactly what he needed at present.

“If you need any immortal artifact, do not hesitate to tell me, Fellow Daoist. I can forge anything.”

“I don’t need anything for now. Should the need arise in the future, I will have to trouble you, Fellow Daoist Daoist Heaven Mandate.”

“Would you like to meet Little Ling and Lianyi?”

“The two younger sisters-in-law? I haven’t seen them in a long time.” Previously, the Heaven Mandate Immortal was worried that Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi might be connected to the Silent Annihilation and hadn’t dared to reveal himself. Now that the misunderstanding was resolved, he naturally wished to meet them.

“Immortal Fairy, do you know where they are?” Lu Yang was befuddled. He remembered that Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi often roamed the outside world, their whereabouts ever-changing.

“Oh, right. I don’t know where they are. Shall we wait until we run into them?”

Yun Zhi shook her head helplessly and produced a yellow talisman. “I was concerned about Senior Ao Ling being attacked by the Gray Immortal Fairy, so I asked Founding Ancestor Vast Sea to craft several Teleportation Talismans. You can use this talisman to find Senior Ao Ling.”

“That would be wonderful! Lass Yun is truly reliable.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy broke into a wide smile. Since Little Ling and Lianyi are often together, finding one will lead to the other.

“I observe that this small world prevents teleportation. It would be best to leave this place before attempting,” Yun reminded them.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal hadn’t expected Yun Zhi to possess such keen discernment. Fearing that the Silent Annihilation Immortal would use some unknown method to teleport directly into the ancient ring’s small world, he had deliberately sealed off all means of teleportation when forging the ancient ring.

After Yun Zhi's departure, Lu Yang also left the ancient ring’s small world. He picked up the fallen ring, slipped it onto his finger, activated the Teleportation Talisman, and was transported to Ao Ling’s location.

Only after watching Lu Yang depart with two immortals did Yun Zhi descend the mountain to find Yun Mengmeng among the river spirits encircling the Dao-Seeking Sect.

Yun Mengmeng lay in the river, letting the current carry her wherever it pleased. This was her latest pastime. In the Peach Blossom Secret Domain, the rivers were immortal rivers, and her granny forbade her from playing like this.

Yun Zhi fished Yun Mengmeng out of the water. “Mengmeng, do you need an immortal artifact forged?”

“Immortal artifact?” Yun Mengmeng pondered. According to the First-in-Command, our Everlasting Lineage has no need for weapons in combat. If I don’t need a weapon-type immortal artifact...

“Will any immortal artifact do?”

“Yes, any will do.”

Yun Mengmeng’s eyes lit up. “Can you forge a treasure bowl? The kind where you put in one pastry, and it turns into two!”

“We can try that.”

Yun Zhi then found Gan Tian lying under a tree and comprehending nature.

“Third Junior Sister, if you could have an immortal artifact forged, what would you like?”

Gan Tian sat up. She didn’t understand why Yun Zhi was asking, but still leaned against the tree and contemplated seriously.

A qin? I don’t really need it. My qin is a growth-type magical treasure, and I’ve used it since childhood. There’s no need to replace it.

“Is there an immortal lamp that can grant any wish? For example, one could rub it twice, and a divine spirit will appear to fulfil three wishes.”

“We can try that.”

Yun Zhi went around asking, jotting down the requested immortal artifacts on paper. “A treasure bowl that duplicates anything; an immortal lamp that grants wishes when rubbed; a coffin so comfortable you never want to get out; an immortal artifact that teaches one about love; an immortal mirror that ranks the most beautiful women...

“They seem to be a bunch of peculiar curios. But considering that Fellow Daoist is the Progenitor and can forge any magical treasure, it shouldn’t be a problem.”

The Demon Region, the ancient tomb of a great demon...

Within a passage, eight or nine demons gathered, and they walked carefully, afraid to take long strides.

“Big Brother, can’t we use our spiritual consciousness to probe our surroundings? It’s so dark here; I can’t see anything.” A female demon clutched at the leader’s clothes nervously.

“How many times have I said this? This is the ancient tomb of a Half-Step Nascent Soul great demon. It’s riddled with mechanisms and traps, including some triggered by spiritual consciousness and light.”

“Oh.”

“Everyone, step only where I’ve stepped, lest you trigger any mechanism.” The leader of the group transmitted an auditory message to the rest.

After triggering two mechanisms and fighting through several fierce battles, the group finally reached the depths of the tomb, a place no one had ever reached.

“We’ve finally made it.” Everyone heaved a sigh of relief and slumped to the ground.

Luminous pearls lit the depths of the ancient tomb. The group saw cultivation manuals, pills, and spirit treasures scattered haphazardly on the floor.

“As expected of a Half-Step Nascent great demon’s bequest. It’s truly abundant.”

“Many thanks to Fellow Daoist Ao Lianyi and Fellow Daoist Jiang Ling for the map of the ancient tomb. As agreed, we’ll split it forty-sixty.”

They had encountered Ao Lianyi and Jiang Ling by chance on the road. The two possessed the map of the ancient tomb but lacked manpower and feared the dangers within. Thus, they had suggested a collaboration—explore the ancient tomb together, then split the spoils forty-sixty.

Through their journey together, the group had learned that the two women came from extraordinary backgrounds. Traces of the Dragon Tribe and the Phoenix Tribe blood ran in their veins, suggesting that their ancestors had been dragons and phoenixes. However, their ancestors were likely expelled after some transgression. By Ao Lianyi and Jiang Ling’s generation, their families had long since declined.

The leader inventoried the treasures in the ancient tombs. Most pills had lost their efficacy over time, turning poisonous or useless. The spirit treasures and cultivation manuals, however, remained usable and were of exceptionally high grades.

“Look at this book! It’s the great demon’s unique spell!”

“And this book! This documents the tomb owner’s experiences of forming a golden core! Heavens, with his method, we’ll have a fifteen percent chance of successfully forming one!”

“Strange. What’s this?” One of them spotted a sword hilt protruding from the ground and pulled it out in curiosity.

“A broken sword? And it's rusty?”

As the rusted blade was pulled out, the ancient tomb shook. The coffin began to rattle, and an eerie laughter echoed through the tomb.

“Heh heh heh, who would have thought? A hundred years have passed, and even I, the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest, am finally seeing the light of day once more!

“Old Black Goat, you sacrificed your life to seal me here and set traps to keep others from entering. But so what? Someone ultimately barged in and removed the array’s focal point!”

“Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest?” The group was flabbergasted, color draining from their faces.

The Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest was a renowned great demon from a century ago. Due to his exceptional cultivation, he was unbeatable, terrorizing the nearby tribes. Later, the old ancestor of the Black Goat Tribe broke through to the Half-Step Nascent Soul Stage and challenged him to battle. Since then, both had vanished.

To think he has been sealed here all this time...

A mottled centipede crawled out of the coffin, its hundred legs swaying in a rhythmic motion that sent chills down one’s spine.

“It appears that after that old Black Goat died, his storage ring shattered, and its contents fell out. Excellent! Not only will this esteemed one have these little ones to replenish my blood and qi upon awakening, but I will also acquire the possessions of a Half-Step Nascent Soul cultivator. The heavens truly favor me!”

“We brought disaster upon you with our map. You must flee! The two of us will bring up the rear.” The two women stepped forward, their eyes flickering with a resolve to sacrifice themselves for their cause.

“Fellow Daoist Ao Lianyi!”

“Fellow Daoist Jiang Ling!”

The group wept. “If we’re leaving, it’ll be together!”

“Shut up! If you don’t leave, we’ll all die here!”

“Hehe, none of you are going anywhere.” The Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest cackled, dismissing the group of Great Perfection Qi Training minor demons before him.

The group engaged the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest in an all-out battle. The latter toyed with them, not bothering to kill them right off the outset.

“Fellow Daoist Ao Lianyi!” the group cried out.

One of the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest’s legs had pierced Ao Lianyi’s chest. She lay weakly in Jiang Ling’s arms.

With a trembling hand stained with blood, Ao Lianyi caressed Jiang Ling’s face. “Little Ling, let us be good sisters again in the next life.”

“Lianyi—” Jiang Ling screamed, her anguish nearly rending her.

“Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest, I will make you pay with blood!” Jiang Ling yelled in fury.

The Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest paid her no mind until he saw her eyes. His expression shifted, and he involuntarily stepped back.

Jiang Ling’s eyes had transformed into golden phoenix eyes, like those of an ancient phoenix awakening.

“Bloodline awakening! You are of the Phoenix Tribe’s direct lineage!” the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest exclaimed.

Suddenly, a door opened. Lu Yang stepped out and surveyed the scene. He saw Ao Ling on the ground, devoid of vitality; Jiang Lianyi, blazing with rage; a group of little demons, bewildered by what had happened; and the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest, a Half-Step Nascent Soul cultivator.

“Sorry. Wrong place.”

Then Lu Yang quietly withdrew.

Chapter 971: Big Brother Heaven Mandate

Lu Yang retreated half a step, then realized that leaving now was too late, so he stepped back in.

“Who are you?!” The Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest’s expression shifted drastically when he saw Lu Yang create a stable spatial channel. What level of powerhouse is he?

“He... he is Lu Yang!” With how far and wide Lu Yang’s reputation had spread, even his portraits had been circulated within the Demon Region.

Sealed away for a hundred years, the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest naturally didn’t recognize Lu Yang, but that didn’t stop the fear from setting in.

“Leave quickly, all of you. Let me handle this.” Lu Yang shooed the minor demons.

Noticing that their eyes kept darting toward Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, he added, “There’s still hope for her; I can save her. Go now.”

Only then did the little demons hastily flee the ancient tomb.

“Seniors, stop crying, and stop sleeping. Get up, please,” Lu Yang said helplessly.

Had I known what transpired here, I would have arrived a little later.

Now, it’s too late for all that.

With Law Follows Words, Lu Yang brought Ao Ling back to life. She sprang up nimbly from the ground. “Senior Brother, what brings you here?”

Jiang Lianyi stopped crying and no longer deliberately emanated her phoenix aura.

“I found an old friend who wishes to meet both of you.” Lu Yang raised his hand to show the black ancient ring. “Right here.”

“Which friend?” The two women were curious. It was rare for them to meet an acquaintance from their era.

“It’s the Heaven Mandate Immortal.” Lu Yang didn’t keep them in suspense.

“Big Brother Heaven Mandate?!” The two were astonished, their faces lighting up with joy. They had thought that Lu Yang might have found someone like the Little Qiongqi, not the Heaven Mandate Immortal, who was extremely close to their husband.

“But should we first deal with this Golden Core centipede?” Lu Yang’s gaze fell upon the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest. “What has this centipede done?”

“We happened to pass by and heard that this centipede had committed many evil deeds and was sealed here, so we decided we might as well get rid of it.”

Lu Yang remarked inwardly, And you decided you might as well recruit a group of minor demons to set the scene and build up the atmosphere, did you? But since you are my junior sisters, I won’t call you out.

The Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest shuddered. Although he didn’t know the trio’s true identities, he could feel the oppressive pressure exuding from them.

Even the Intermediate Nascent Soul old demon I encountered more than a century ago didn’t possess a fraction of the terrifying oppressive pressure these three emit.

And did I just hear him mention the Heaven Mandate Immortal?

Who is he? An ancient immortal playing around in the mortal realm?

Did I violate some heavenly law? Why do I have to be hunted, besieged, and intercepted by three mighty experts the moment I emerge?

I’m a Half-Step Nascent Soul cultivator, not a half-immortal!

Had I known, I would have continued sleeping in the coffin.

“You three...”

Lu Yang levitated the broken half of a sword from the ground, infused it with a wisp of sword qi, and casually tossed it behind him. It pierced the Heavenly Centipede Daoist Priest’s brow. With two strangled sounds, the centipede collapsed into the coffin, fitting neatly inside.

The two women and Lu Yang entered the ancient ring. There stood the Heaven Mandate Immortal at the edge of a precipice overlooking the ocean, one hand behind his back and the other hanging naturally at his side. His white robes billowed in the sea breeze as endless waves crashed against the shore.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal turned, his gaze steeped in the vicissitudes of three hundred millennia. “Two younger sisters-in-law, it has been a long time; three hundred thousand years. How have you fared?”

Even without seeing his face, his bearing alone told the two women who he was. Overjoyed, they exclaimed, “Big Brother Heaven Mandate!”

Separated for over three hundred thousand years, their reunion in this small world filled them with a joy beyond words.

“Big Brother Heaven Mandate, we never expected you to be here!”

“You two are as close as biological sisters, just like in ancient times.”

“Big Brother Heaven Mandate, what exactly happened back then? Where have you been all these years?”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal gazed upward. “It’s a complicated story. Sit, all of you. I’ll explain slowly.”

He controlled the small world, conjuring five chairs, and invited them to sit and enjoy the sea breeze.

“Here’s what happened back then...”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal recounted the tale from the beginning, omitting the details of Hui Doudou, merely mentioning that they had encountered a powerful enemy they couldn’t defeat and had been forced into hiding.

Jiang Lianyi was unaware of Hui Doudou’s existence. If the Heaven Mandate Immortal told her, she would also end up on the hit list.

“To think so much has happened. It sounds like you’ve also endured much hardship these three hundred thousand years, Big Brother Heaven Mandate.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal had hidden in the ancient ring’s small world, searching for trustworthy cultivators to gather intelligence while waiting for the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s resurrection. It was certainly far from as easy as he made it sound.

“Aiya, I won’t call that hardship. I still manifested myself before people daily, just as before.”

“And the one who had his body taken over...” The two women hesitated, not daring to finish the sentence. If it were the Qilin Immortal who had his body taken over, what then?

The Heaven Mandate Immortal fell silent. He couldn’t pat his chest and guarantee that the one who had his body taken over wasn’t the Qilin Immortal. If it truly was the Qilin Immortal who had his body taken over, and the two women trusted him, only to fall victim, what then?

“Don’t worry, it’s fine! Lu Yang and I will definitely find the one whose body has been taken over!” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy stepped forward to ease the tension.

Lu Yang looked at the Everlasting Immortal Fairy as if he had seen a ghost. Immortal Fairy, you can say what you like, but can you not drag me into it? With my puny frame, do you think I’d dare get involved with the mastermind?

“That’s right. The Immortal Fairy and I will definitely find who it is!” Knowing the Everlasting Immortal Fairy meant well, Lu Yang went along with it.

The two women had witnessed Lu Yang’s skill and luck before and were confident he was absolutely reliable. “Then we’ll have to thank you in advance, Everlasting-jiejie and Senior Brother Lu Yang.”

Next, the Heaven Mandate Immortal recounted his encounter with the Specter Lord, then with Lu Yang and the Everlasting Immortal Fairy, eventually following Lu Yang to the Dao-Seeking Sect, where he met Yun Zhi.

When the two women heard that he had offered to let Yun Zhi have three moves, they involuntarily sat up straight.

We’ve got to give it to Big Brother Heaven Mandate; even Everlasting-jiejie in her prime wouldn’t dare offer to grant Yun Zhi three moves, would she?

“Ahem, don’t look at me like that. I genuinely didn’t expect her to be that formidable.” It was rare for the Heaven Mandate Immortal to be embarrassed, and he averted his gaze. “I’d assumed she was at most at the same level as Qilin. Who would have thought that she would be this powerful? Looking back, she must be Yun Mengmeng, the creed leader of the Heavenly Court Creed.”

The two women glared indignantly at the Heaven Mandate Immortal. “When you fought our husband all the time, you never offered him three moves.”

“Slip of the tongue, slip of the tongue.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal smiled ingratiatingly. He quickly changed the subject. “By the way, I heard that Zhu Tian is now the monarch of the Demon Kingdom. I didn’t expect him to have also slumbered until now. My small world has been lacking furniture; this is a perfect chance to have him craft some.”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal could craft furniture by reshaping the small world, or he could also ask Lu Yang to buy some from the outside world, but in terms of comfort, nothing could match Zhu Tian’s craftsmanship.

“Who would have imagined Zhu Tian to still be alive in this era? When Lianyi told me he was still around, I thought she was joking.” Ao Ling chuckled.

Since Zhu Tian was under the influence of Jiang Lianyi’s Law of the Jungle Incipient Dao Fruit, she could sense his location. She pinched her fingers to focus. “Zhu Tian is in the Demon Kingdom’s capital. Shall we go over now?”

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy had revealed herself to Zhu Tian before, and she was aware of Lu Yang’s true identity. There was no need for them to be secretive when meeting him.

Chapter 972: Dismantling The Act

Demon Region, Demon City’s imperial palace...

Since the establishment of the Demon Kingdom, tens of thousands of demons had come to pay homage. Major and small tribes had submitted, bringing unprecedented unity to the Demon Region.

Under the influence of the Law of the Jungle Incipient Dao Fruit, over a dozen Transcendent Tribulation demon emperors had pledged allegiance to Zhu Tian. Several great demons at the Apex Fusion Stage had also received his tutelage and had the possibility of reaching the Transcendent Tribulation Stage.

“This is the life.” Zhu Tian reclined on his monarch chair and let out a comfortable yawn. He felt that he hadn’t slumbered for three hundred thousand years in vain.

If this were in the ancient era, how could I have enjoyed such good fortune?

The palace hall was lavishly decorated, even more opulent than the Great Xia imperial palace, surpassing it in both artistic and practical terms.

Zhu Tian had personally designed the Demon City’s imperial palace, and every piece of furniture within had been crafted by his own hands. When he first awoke and had yet to return to his peak state, he crafted furniture in his cave abode when he had nothing to do. Once the imperial palace was built, he put them all to use.

The vast palace hall was empty, save for Zhu Tian. He needed no maidservants or eunuchs to attend to him, much less guards to protect him.

As an almighty half-immortal in my own territory, who could possibly harm me?

“Zhu Tian, you seem to be having quite the comfortable time here.” A teasing voice sounded from behind him, startling him so badly that he nearly reverted to his original form.

Recognizing the visitor, Zhu Tian quickly plastered on a smile. “So it’s Sister Lianyi, Sister Ling, and Big Brother Lu Yang.”

“And this immortal.” The Everlasting Immortal Fairy darted out from within Lu Yang.

“Yes, yes, yes, and Sister Doudou.” Zhu Tian would never forget this great immortal. Although the Everlasting Immortal Fairy had never revealed her origins, it hardly mattered. No immortal was someone he could afford to offend.

“What brings the four of you here?” Zhu Tian promptly rose to make room, pouring tea and setting out melon seeds.

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi had visited him more than once, but Lu Yang hadn’t come since the Demon Kingdom’s founding ceremony. He had often entertained the idea of sending several beautiful female demon dancers to Lu Yang to get into his good books. However, he had hesitated, worried that such a direct gesture might come across poorly, so the idea was shelved.

“An old acquaintance would like to meet you. Would that be convenient, Monarch Zhu?”

“Just call me Little Zhu.” Zhu Tian bowed and scraped, not daring to say that it was inconvenient.

“In here; please enter, Monarch Zhu.” Lu Yang showed the black ancient ring, whose immense suction drew everyone into its small world.

Entering the small world, Zhu Tian felt as though he had been transported back to the ancient era. Countless familiar half-immortals and Transcendent Tribulation cultivators sat listening to a lecture. Before them was the Heaven Mandate Immortal, clad in snow-white robes. He spoke with eloquence, and his words triggered unusual phenomena. The sound of Dao echoed through the air, and fountains of golden light sprang from the earth, enlightening all.

Zhu Tian had recollections of this lecture. It was the first lecture the Heaven Mandate Immortal had given after becoming an immortal. Why is it appearing here?

Could it be that this lecture is so significant that it has been etched into history, becoming a historical projection and reappearing in this small world?!

The Heaven Mandate Immortal descended from the platform, lotuses blooming beneath each footstep. Wherever he passed, the projections of everyone dissolved into white mist and vanished.

This scene didn't belong to Zhu Tian’s memory.

Could the Heaven Mandate Immortal's cultivation be so profound that he can cross the river of time, reappearing in the mortal realm through mere historical projections?

Step by step, the Heaven Mandate Immortal walked towards the stunned Zhu Tian, a meaningful smile playing at his lips.

“Zhu Tian, it has been a long time. Has everything been well?”

“Immortal Venerable, it really is you?!” Zhu Tian suddenly registered that the person before him was no historical projection but the actual, living Heaven Mandate Immortal!

The Heaven Mandate Immortal nodded with a smile. “Of course it is me.”

Lu Yang clicked his tongue as he listened from the side. As expected of the Heaven Mandate Immortal. Zhu Tian had been among the most attentive half-immortals during his lectures, yet he could still be hoodwinked. If it were anyone else, wouldn't it be far worse?

“I didn't expect you to choose to slumber, Zhu Tian. In a flash, three hundred thousand years have passed, and I have found no acquaintances in this vast world. Now that I have met you today, this journey is worthwhile.”

Tears welled up in Zhu Tian’s eyes. That's right. I've already slept for three hundred thousand years, yet I still can't escape from you all.

It's one thing for Jiang Lianyi and Ao Ling to appear one after another, but now, even the Heaven Mandate Immortal has shown up.

When I stayed hidden, not a single half-immortal showed themselves. The moment I established the Demon Kingdom, half-immortals began popping up one after another, with the occasional immortal appearing every now and then.

There are now more immortals than in ancient times. Can you give me—the number one below immortals—a little room to shine?

At this rate, maybe even the Qilin Immortal would pop up next.

Wait, aren't there currently rumors about the Battle of the Great Era?

Could I have triggered the Battle of the Great Era?

At that thought, Zhu Tian’s heart stirred with excitement. I am indeed the most special.

“Immortal Venerable, please sit.” Zhu Tian brought out several monarch chairs, all newly crafted within the last two years.

After everyone sat down, Zhu Tian took out a monarch table and thoughtfully laid out fruits and pastries.

“Zhu Tian, you look to be doing quite well in the Demon Region. You've even established a Demon Kingdom.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal smiled.

“It’s all thanks to the support of my fellow Daoists,” Zhu Tian said humbly.

It was indeed due to their support; otherwise, Jiang Lianyi would have become the monarch of the Demon Kingdom.

“That, too, was because you had skill. In my opinion, now that you've established the Demon Kingdom, uniting the demon forces is a wise move.

“The Law of the Jungle Incipient Dao Fruit inherently strengthens its wielder. Lianyi is used to working solo and has never attempted it. As for you, you had no chance to attempt it in the ancient era.”

In those days, Transcendent Tribulation cultivators and half-immortals ran rampant. If Zhu Tian had wished to establish the Demon Kingdom, not only would he have had the Qilin Immortal above him, but several other half-immortal demons wouldn't have agreed to it.

“The current half-immortals keep a low profile for various reasons, giving you the chance to establish the Demon Kingdom. You should cherish this fortuitous opportunity and properly figure out your Law of the Jungle Incipient Dao Fruit.”

“I, Zhu Tian, will keep your teachings in mind, Immortal Venerable.” Cold sweat broke out on Zhu Tian's brow. Since awakening, he had been indulging in pleasure and neglecting his cultivation.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal rose, clasping his hands behind his back as he circled the group. Suddenly, he asked, “Zhu Tian, what do you think of this small world I refined?”

Zhu Tian buttered him up. “Immortal Venerable, anything refined by you is naturally of the best quality. This small world is tranquil and elegant, devoid of all things, embodying the spirit platform's empty, clear meaning.

Lu Yang, however, held a different opinion. “A small world is good, but it feels a bit empty. It doesn't seem suited for living.”

“The empty and clear meaning of the spirit platform is indeed a good state of mind. However, you have already attained this realm, Immortal Venerable. Why use the small world to serve as a foil? Why don't I craft a set of furniture for you to adorn this small world?”

The Heaven Mandate Immortal nodded. “That works too.”

The three exchanged a smile, the atmosphere harmonious.

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy squatted on the monarch chair, watching the three laugh for some time. She couldn't help but feel that there was more to the exchange than met the eye, and she contemplated carefully.

Right then, understanding flashed in the Everlasting Immortal Fairy's eyes, and she pointed at the Heaven Mandate Immortal. “Oh~ I get it! Heaven Mandate, you're embarrassed to ask Zhu Tian directly for furniture, so you enlisted Xiao Yangzi to be your shill!”

“...” The Heaven Mandate Immortal was silent. Everlasting, why is this the only time you're clever?

Next

Chapter 973: Can You People From The Dao-Seeking Sect Ask For Normal Immortal Artifacts?

At times like this, the Heaven Mandate Immortal would find himself longing for the reticent Everlasting Immortal Fairy of bygone days.

Back then, she would see through things but hold her tongue. Now, it was uncertain whether she would see through things, but if she did, she would certainly not hold her tongue.

This was not the first time the Heaven Mandate Immortal had his act dismantled. He quickly recovered, defusing the awkwardness with laughter. “Haha, Everlasting, you really know how to jest. All my life, I’ve been upright and aboveboard. When have I ever needed to be secretive or cover up anything?”

“Ah? Haven’t you? What about that time you covered up being captured by female cultivators to be harvested...”

Lu Yang lunged forward like an arrow to cover the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s mouth. If I let her continue speaking, she’ll be fine even if she exposes all his deepest secrets, but Zhu Tian and I, mere bystanders, will be in for disaster.

Besides, I’ve worked so hard to serve as the Heaven Mandate Immortal’s shill, hoping that he would spare my life when I encounter my heavenly tribulation. But now, it seems I won’t be able to survive.

Nearby, Zhu Tian was sweating profusely. Wait, how did this casual conversation suddenly plunge me into a life-and-death crisis?

I didn’t do anything at all.

The Everlasting Immortal Fairy was a soul entity, so Lu Yang couldn’t physically touch her. Still, when she saw him charging toward her with his hand raised, she cooperatively fell silent.

The sight of this made Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi cover their mouths, stifling their laughter.

Apart from the Everlasting Immortal Fairy’s unintentional sabotage, the atmosphere within the ancient ring’s small world was harmonious and joyous.

It was rare for these ancient cultivators to gather. They reminisced about the ancient era, and even Lu Yang’s presence didn’t seem out of place. In fact, Lu Yang knew more about ancient matters than Zhu Tian did.

Of course, Lu Yang couldn’t pretend he knew everything. He “forgot” everything about the Four Ancient Immortals’ humiliating experiences. To do otherwise would invite calamity.

In a flash, two days and nights passed. For mortals, going without rest would be exhausting. However, everyone present was either a half-immortal or an immortal; even the token Divine Transfiguration cultivator felt no fatigue.

Nevertheless, their conversation couldn’t continue indefinitely; all things would come to an end.

“Alright, that’s enough. It’s hard to meet old friends. I simply came by to visit you. It’s good that you are well.” The Heaven Mandate Immortal took the initiative to conclude their conversation.

“Immortal Venerable, in that case, once I complete this set of furniture, I will deliver it to you.”

“You don’t have to be so courteous.”

“It’s only proper.”

After seeing off Lu Yang and the others, Zhu Tian grew even more convinced than ever that he must cling to Lu Yang’s thigh and stay in his good graces.

“During the conversation, the Immortal Venerable kept calling Big Brother Lu Yang ‘young friend.’ In ancient times, I never heard him address anyone like that. It appears that Big Brother Lu Yang has earned his favor; he is destined for great things.”

Although I was the one who triggered the Battle of the Great Era and am fated to be the protagonist of this world, I need to know when to go with the flow and choose sides when I should.

“Come to think of it, why can people come and go as they please in my imperial palace? Should I hire a few guards?”

Then he soon realized something. What level of guards could possibly stop Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi?

“Senior Brother Lu Yang, let us part here.” Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi said goodbye to Lu Yang, while the latter returned to the Dao-Seeking Sect with the Everlasting Immortal Fairy and the Heaven Mandate Immortal.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal looked at the list of immortal artifacts that Yun Zhi had written, bewilderment crossing his face.

Logically speaking, when seeking me to forge immortal artifacts, shouldn’t the requests be for weapon-type immortal artifacts, and the more powerful, the better?

What kind of absurd immortal artifacts are listed in this paper?

The Heaven Mandate Immortal stared at the list for a long while, searching for something normal within.

“A treasure bowl that can duplicate anything and an immortal lamp that grants any wish when rubbed. Aren’t these effects too heaven-defying?

“A coffin so comfortable you never want to get out—you should ask Zhu Tian for this! The only coffin I can craft is one you can’t escape!

“And an immortal artifact that teaches one about love. I’ve never been in love myself. Where would I begin to forge such a thing?

“An immortal mirror that ranks the most beautiful women...”

Can you people from the Dao-Seeking Sect ask for immortal artifacts with normal functions?

“Are you unable to forge them?” Yun Zhi asked.

The Heaven Mandate Immortal covered his face. Although he didn’t want to admit it, he genuinely couldn’t forge these things.

At least ask me to reforge the Heaven-Splitting Axe.

“If we require any more immortal artifacts, we will contact you again, Fellow Daoist.”

“My artifact refinement skills are average, not as formidable as you imagine, Fellow Daoist Yun Zhi. I’ll have to trouble you to limit future requests to weapon-type immortal artifacts.”

“I will try my best.”

“One more thing. The immortal palace directly performed a soul search on the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord. The intelligence gathered matches our speculation—the Midheaven Emperor-Lord possesses the Devouring Dao Fruit. It allows him to devour other Dao Fruits for his own use, though it places extremely high demands on one’s soul strength.”

Lu Yang had found it easy to teleport to the Demon Region, but returning proved difficult. Combined with the time spent conversing with Zhu Tian, the round trip had taken seven days.

Over these seven days, the immortal sects and the imperial court had paid extra attention to extracting intelligence from the Demon-Subduing Divine Lord and the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord.

“The method to undo the immortal palace’s Great Sect Defense Array was leaked by the former palace leader, Qu Ling. Outraged, the immortal palace has executed her.

“As for the method and principle to craft the four immortal-searching tokens, the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord is unaware. He has only recently advanced to the half-immortal status and is yet to be apprised of all the Great Qian Dynasty’s high-level intelligence. He only knows that the immortal-searching tokens are precious, that even the Midheaven Emperor-Lord possesses just a few, and that only especially powerful Great Qian Immortals, such as the Specter Lord and the Mountain-River Lord, were deemed worthy to hold them.

“Although the Mountain-River Lord possessed the Devouring Incipient Dao Fruit, it was not bestowed upon him by the Midheaven Emperor-Lord. In fact, it was the opposite—he was once the Midheaven Emperor-Lord’s rival in the contest for immortality.”

“The Mountain-River Lord was actually that powerful?” Lu Yang was astonished. In the East Sea, the Mountain-River Lord had first fought Ao Ling, then been instantly slain by Hui Doudou. From start to finish, he had never had a chance to show his true strength, leaving Lu Yang with the impression that he couldn’t even defeat Zhu Tian.

“According to the Devil-Vanquishing Divine Lord, Great Qian’s forces have two immortals—the ‘Midheaven Emperor-Lord,’ who everyone knows, and the ‘Fate Arbiter.’[1]

“The Great Qian Dynasty was built upon faith and incense offerings. This Fate Arbiter presided over sacrificial rites, ranking only below the Midheaven Emperor-Lord.”

“I see.” Lu Yang nodded.

“As for why the Midheaven Emperor-Lord allowed the Great Qian Dynasty to fall, it stemmed from the flaws of faith power. Faith power bound all cultivators who held divine positions, including the Midheaven Emperor-Lord himself. Many were assimilated by it.

“Therefore, the Midheaven Emperor-Lord decided to abandon the Great Qian Dynasty, eradicate its source of incense offerings, and let it march toward ruin.”

“That’s truly heartless.” Lu Yang sighed. The Great Qian Dynasty’s path to destruction hadn’t happened overnight. Generations upon generations of civilians had suffered under exploitative officials and the hardships of cultivator warfare. From praying to those in divine positions to cursing and abandoning them—this was exactly what the Midheaven Emperor-Lord and the others wished for.

“Speaking of which, Wu Yao also personally destroyed the dynasty he established.”

“The founders of two great dynasties brought about their dynasties’ destruction with their own hands. I wonder if it was a coincidence or fate,” Lu Yang said, sighing with emotion.

1. Siming is also the name of a Chinese deity who presides over lifespan and fate. ☜

Chapter 974: Shared Fortune And Adversity

After escorting the Heaven Mandate Immortal, whose artifact refinement skills were merely average, back to the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace, Lu Yang’s cultivation resumed its proper course, and he devoted himself to it with single-minded focus.

An hour later, a conniving sycophant appeared, disrupting Lu Yang’s cultivation plans.

“Old Lu, I heard you went to the immortal palace?”

“Why? Envious, aren’t you?” Lu Yang snorted smugly, causing Meng Jingzhou to gnash his teeth in indignation.

Ever since the sun’s power intensified, Meng Jingzhou had grown increasingly wary of approaching women. A place like the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace was practically a forbidden zone for him.

Meng Jingzhou had been mystified when Lu Yang vanished into thin air. Only upon asking around did he learn that Lu Yang had abandoned him and gone off to the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace to enjoy himself.

Meng Jingzhou couldn’t bear to watch Lu Yang sink into such decadence. He berated him harshly, hoping to jolt his wayward friend back to reason. “Envy my ass! We cultivators should devote ourselves to cultivation with a single-minded focus. Someone like you, who spends his days wandering about idly, will definitely never amount to anything!”

“And what about you? Have you cultivated this past month?”

“More or less. While you were gone over the past month, I went to a secret domain that opens once every three hundred years and happened to bump into Long Huo. From subtle clues, I deduced that the secret domain’s spirit was its owner. I thwarted his body takeover scheme and fought him. Though his cultivation realm was higher than mine, I have a natural advantage against soul entities. After overcoming the gap, I joined forces with Long Huo to capture him.

“Afterward, I handed the soul over to the local authorities. While waiting for their commendation, I noticed they were exceptionally efficient, almost eager to chase me away. Upon investigating secretly, I discovered traces of corruption within the local authorities and their collusion with the Heartless Creed. Following that lead, I uncovered that the prefect was the source.

“When the prefect realized his plot was exposed, he scurried away that night. Amid a torrential downpour, I pursued him for a thousand li, cutting down the sacrificial soldiers he dispatched and ignoring the honey trap of the beauties he sent after me. I chased him until dawn, finally pinning him down at a city gate.

“Looking up, I realized my pursuit had led me all the way to the imperial city. After dealing with the prefect, I stopped by my house for a visit. Upon returning, I heard you had come back from the immortal palace.”

“So...when did you cultivate in the past month?”

Meng Jingzhou said with a straight face, “At the very least, I had maintained the heart to cultivate. It was merely that mundane affairs had kept me from finding the opportunity.”

“Me too!” Lu Yang fumed. As if you’re the only one tangled by mundane affairs.

“Yeah, right.”

“Old Meng, let me tell you—you’d better watch your tone. You still have time to cozy up to me now. It’ll be too late if you want to in the future.”

Meng Jingzhou dismissed his words with contempt. “Me, cozy up to you? Dream on.”

“You don’t know, do you? During this trip to the immortal palace, I didn’t just encounter Great Qian cultivators; I even met the Heaven Mandate Immortal. When he saw me, he was extremely grateful and patted me on the shoulder, encouraging me to cultivate diligently. He promised to take good care of me when I undergo my heavenly tribulation.”

“What?!” Meng Jingzhou was absolutely floored. To think that Lu Yang had obtained such a fortuitous opportunity while I wasn’t with him.

Which cultivator isn’t afraid of heavenly tribulations? Even the mighty Qin Haoran had met his demise during his heavenly tribulation, reincarnating as Junior Brother Li. With Lu Yang’s connection to the Heaven Mandate Immortal, wouldn’t he be free from the greatest obstacle to cultivation, able to soar to the skies unimpeded?

Meng Jingzhou solicitously began massaging Lu Yang’s shoulders and head, his tone softening. “Old Lu—ah, no, Senior Brother Lu—I might have spoken a bit harshly just now. Please forgive me. You know, considering our relationship...”

Lu Yang was now a person of standing. As the Second-in-Command of the Great Dou Dynasty, how could he lower himself to petty squabbles with Meng Jingzhou? “Our relationship goes without saying. We’ve always shared fortune and adversity. Don’t worry, when I meet Senior Heaven Mandate Immortal again, I’ll make sure he takes good care of you, too.”

Meng Jingzhou was deeply moved. “Good brother! You’re truly loyal!”

“By the way, on my way back, I also heard that several of the immortal palace’s higher-ups and disciples have become pregnant after your visit to the immortal palace. People are saying that it was your doing. Do you know about that?”

“Rubbish!” Lu Yang’s eyes reddened with anger. All these rumors are outright slander! Does this have anything to do with me? It’s the Twins River water that caused their pregnancies. What did I do? I only gave the immortal palace a suggestion.

“Wait... Putting it that way, this matter does have something to do with me.”

Hearing this, Meng Jingzhou, whose hands were massaging Lu Yang’s shoulder, immediately grabbed his shoulders, hurling him away. His eyes blazed with fury.

“Well, well, Old Lu! I can’t believe I misjudged you. To think you’d be capable of such filthy acts! Show your hand; I must teach you a proper lesson today!”

“No, I meant I merely gave them a suggestion to drink from the Twins River...”

A golden swastika-symbol fist seal that was ten zhang across came crashing down upon him. Lu Yang formed a sword with his fingers and cleaved it apart. “Damn it, Old Meng, you’re pretending not to hear me, aren’t you—”

“Lecherous scoundrel, take this!

“It’s the perfect chance to let you witness the Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation I learned from my ancestor!”

Behind Meng Jingzhou, a golden Dharma Manifestation resembling the great sun slowly rose. It towered a hundred zhang, its features indistinct. Every one of its movements was imbued with the aura of primordial ages past. It wielded a shield in one hand and a halberd in the other.

“Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation?” Lu Yang had heard of the Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation, which was exclusive to those with a Pure Yang Immortal Physique. But this looks like an even more extreme version, likely tailored by Meng Junzi for Meng Jingzhou.

Boom!

One arm of the Dharma Manifestation swept down, sending Lu Yang flying away. Lu Yang was certain now. This is definitely more powerful than the Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation. Based on the Immortal Fairy’s description, it’s not this formidable.

“Do you think I’ll be afraid of you?!” Lu Yang drew the Qingfeng Sword and activated the Immortal-Exterminating Sword Array, pointing its tip at Meng Jingzhou.

Having anticipated this, Meng Jingzhou attacked, lunging at Lu Yang. This way, even if I have to kneel, I can still bring Lu Yang down with my Dharma Manifestation.

Lu Yang inwardly cursed Meng Jingzhou’s shameless act, then changed his sword technique. “Ten Thousand Swords Return to One!”

The Chengying Sword, the Seven-Star Sword Set, and the Qingfeng Sword merged, and sword qi surged explosively. The Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation raised its shield to block, while its other hand swung the halberd at Lu Yang.

“Cosmos in Palm!”

A massive hand descended, clamping down on the Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Manifestation.

Seeing this, Meng Jingzhou executed Three Heads Six Arms, and his Dharma Manifestation followed suit, its six arms stacking together as they blocked the terrifying palm strike.

“Is this all you’ve got?”

“This is more than enough to handle you!”

The two became a tangled knot, their sword moves, spells, and the Dharma Manifestation clashing relentlessly.

Not far away, Yun Mengmeng sat on a tree branch, swinging her legs. She was clearly in a good mood. Every now and then, she would take a dried potato slice from a paper bag and munch on it with relish. She pointed at the dueling pair with fingers covered in potato crumbs. “Shouldn’t someone stop them?”

Seated beside her, Yun Zhi also took a dried potato slice and savored it slowly. “There’s no need to stop them. They’ve always been like that.”

Yun Mengmeng turned to Gan Tian on her left. “Would you like some dried potato slices?”

“No, it’s fine. I shall play my qin to help them liven things up.”

Gan Tian placed her guqin on her lap and began playing an epic melody that reverberated throughout Heaven’s Gate Peak, driving Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou to fight with even greater intensity.

Since Gan Tian’s hands were occupied, Yun Mengmeng would feed her a dried potato slice every now and then.

Chapter 975: Intermediate Divine Transfiguration Stage

Gan Tian’s melody was not only directed at enemies, but it also had supportive properties. As Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou fought to their heart’s content, hearing her melody allowed their strength to soar.

Typically, Meng Jingzhou could only sustain his Dharma Manifestation for a short while, but under the music’s influence, the duration was significantly extended.

Lu Yang’s sword qi and spells seemed inexhaustible, pouring forth without end.

Gan Tian licked a stray potato crumb stuck on her lips. “The dried potato slices taste pretty good, but they lack a bit of flavor. Do you have honey?”

“Yes, yes, I do.” Yun Mengmeng’s storage rings might lack other things, but seasonings were abundant—sesame oil, soy sauce, cumin, chili...

“Here. This is honey I brought from my hometown, made from peach blossoms. It’s delicious.”

Yun Mengmeng produced a small bottle of honey, drizzling it over the potato slices before sealing the paper bag and shaking it vigorously. The thick, golden liquid gave off an immensely sweet fragrance, and its spirit energy concentration was so dense that only those at the Fusion Stage or above could digest it properly.

Gan Tian ate a honey-coated potato slice and nodded with satisfaction. “Mmm, now it tastes right.”

Yun Mengmeng licked the honey from her fingertips. “So sweet. Little Zhi, want some?”

“No, it’s fine.”

Yun Mengmeng looked at her “childish” friend in surprise. “You’re still afraid of tooth decay at this age?”

“I’m just not fond of overly sweet things.”

“I don’t believe you.”

Yun Zhi was rendered speechless.

Two fought, three watched. It was a scene of peace and harmony atop Heaven’s Gate Peak.

“When will this fight end?” Lu Yang transmitted an auditory message to Meng Jingzhou.

“No idea.”

It could be said that their cultivation had no weaknesses. Both possessed formidable offensive power and robust physiques and were unrivalled among those at the same level.

In terms of offensive moves, Lu Yang had the edge; in terms of physical resilience, Meng Jingzhou held the advantage. Now bolstered by Gan Tian’s music, their offensive might and bodily strength were enhanced, and their rate of healing was astonishing.

At this rate, it would be difficult to determine a victor.

And this is so exhausting...

“Y-you, just give up...”

“Y-you should give up...”

“Dream on...”

In the end, they were utterly spent, their movements weak and sluggish. Although they could still muster some spirit energy, they were too exhausted to care, yet neither was willing to concede, so they resorted to throwing punches. Seeing that they could barely continue fighting, Gan Tian kindly stopped playing her qin.

Without the music’s support, the two had no strength left to prop themselves up. They took two steps toward each other, then collapsed, their heads knocking together with a thud before they fell fast asleep on the ground.

From a distance, Yun Zhi performed a spell, levitating Lu Yang back to his cave abode and sending Meng Jingzhou back to his.

With Meng Jingzhou gone, Lu Yang could finally return to cultivating in peace.

Three years passed in a flash.

As agreed upon, Bai Ye relocated a peak from the Great Snow Mountain and settled at the Dao-Seeking Sect. Sharing the same body as her, the Moonchaser True Person came to reside at the sect as well.

Behind Heaven’s Gate Peak now stood the Great Snow Mountain, making it convenient for Lu Yang to visit Bai Ye.

To outsiders unaware of Bai Ye’s existence, they simply assumed that the Moon Laurel Immortal Palace was attempting to transplant moon laurel trees.

After cultivating for so long, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou wanted to go to the outside world for a stroll. Inadvertently, they stumbled upon a string of murders. Investigating the identities of the six victims, they discovered all had been biaoshi armed escorts for a particular shipment. Following this lead, they identified the seventh person, also the last target.

By the time they arrived, the seventh person had already been assassinated. However, using a symbol the victim had traced in blood with his finger in his final moments, along with their formidable spiritual consciousness, the two successfully uncovered an encrypted ledger hidden within a secret compartment in the study.

What followed was an endless stream of pursuers sent to silence Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, all of whom were naturally cut down in turn. These sacrificial soldiers bore restrictions that prevented their souls from being searched.

The two turned their attention back to the ledger. After deciphering it, they discovered it recorded the smuggling operations between the Demon Kingdom and Great Xia, involving a spirit stone shortfall with far-reaching interests.

Lu Yang reasoned that such a vast operation must involve one of the great Transcendent Tribulation demon tribes, all of whom now resided in the Demon Kingdom’s Demon City.

Subsequently, they disguised themselves as rogue cultivators and sneaked into the Demon City. By chance, they bumped into Zhu Tian, who was traveling incognito. After a series of carefully staged events, with Zhu Tian pretending not to know them, they finally identified the Jiuying Tribe as one of the smuggling parties.

The two pretended to pick a fight and wreak havoc in the Jiuying Tribe, fighting their way from the Foundation Establishment members up to forcing a Fusion great demon to take action.

Seeing that they were no match, they feigned escape, luring out the Jiuying Tribe’s Transcendent Tribulation old ancestor to pursue them. At the critical juncture, Zhu Tian intervened and subdued him.

Following the trail, they extracted confessions from the Jiuying Tribe and ascertained that the smuggler from Great Xia was Duke Qin, Jing Yansong.[1] When Great Xia’s imperial court learned of this, they cooperated in the arrest, dismantling Duke Qin’s long-standing smuggling network and forces. The illicit proceeds were then used to replenish the treasuries of both nations.

This case became the largest smuggling scandal since the Demon Kingdom’s founding and was enshrined by the Great Xia’s Ministry of Justice as a model case to be passed down for generations.

During Lu Yang’s clash with the Jiuying Tribe’s Fusion great demon, he had accidentally obtained a fragmented map from the other party’s robes. Poring over ancient texts, he uncovered that it was a key to the legendary Cloudscape Secret Domain, a place said to contain countless treasures. Only by gathering all nine fragments could one unlock it.

Lu Yang also realized that the fragments allowed communication among their owners. All nine pieces of the map had owners, but mutual distrust prevented them from gathering. With a lack of other options, Lu Yang enlisted the help of the Heaven Strategy Sect, who used the fragment in his possession to calculate the locations of the other eight. He then sought them out one by one. Among them, the strongest was at the Fusion Stage, and the weakest was at the Golden Core Stage.

After assembling all the fragments, they opened the Cloudscape Secret Domain. However, it would only admit Golden Core cultivators. Lu Yang had no choice but to request the Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s assistance. Using the power of his Space Incipient Dao Fruit, he loosened the secret domain’s entry conditions, allowing all nine people to enter.

However, it turned out that the Cloudscape Secret Domain was a trap left by a Transcendent Tribulation cultivator from the late Great Qian era. Upon realizing that, the Vast Sea Daoist Lord engaged the Great Qian cultivator and captured him alive.

As for the treasures within the secret domain, the most valuable one was none other than the Great Qian cultivator himself.

Apart from these incidents and a scattering of trivial matters, these three years had passed uneventfully.

Atop Heaven’s Gate Peak, a Spirit-Qi Gathering Array constructed by high-grade spirit stones radiated outward. Lu Yang pressed his hands together and sat cross-legged on a lotus platform formed by nine sharp swords.

Forming seals with his hands, he regulated his breath. His essence, qi, and mind merged into one, like a spirit sword splitting apart the heavens. As his momentum rose, the high-grade spirit stones were rapidly depleted, becoming hollow shells as their power flowed ceaselessly into Lu Yang.

His meridians expanded, his dantian enlarged, and his primordial soul grew vividly lifelike... his body underwent world-shaking transformations.

The momentum that had soared to its zenith suddenly plummeted. Lu Yang exhaled a pure breath. His mind, soul, and body were now purified, with all acquired turbidity expelled.

He stretched, his joints popping and cracking. “At last, Intermediate Divine Transfiguration Stage.”

1. Fun fact: this person was first mentioned in chapter 145. ☜
