With his insight, he couldn’t spot any aspects of Long Arm Boxing that could be modified without disrupting its integrity, indicating that the Cultivator who modified Long Arm Boxing had a Cultivation Level far beyond his own.

“Good morning, Benefactors,” Master Duanchen appeared behind them with a smile, holding a string of Buddha Beads.

“Master.”

“May I know what the Benefactors have planned for today? It will help me arrange things properly,” he asked.

Lu Yang smiled and said, “We’ve just arrived and know very little about Buddha Country, so we have no specific plans. We leave it all to you, Master.”

“Today is the day I receive pilgrims, which can also be considered a major feature of our temple. If the two benefactors are free, why not come and see?”

As the abbot, Master Duanchen has numerous responsibilities and couldn’t possibly meet with pilgrims every day; otherwise, he would accomplish nothing.

Therefore, he established a rule to randomly receive pilgrims on one day within the month, depending entirely on fate.

“Then we will impose upon the master.”

The incense offerings at Kaihuang Temple were more prosperous than under the previous abbots, with many pilgrims waiting at the door even before it opened, including some from other cities.

When the pilgrims learned that today was the day Master Duanchen would be receiving them, they were overjoyed.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou had special identities. Although not everyone knew their faces, their reputations were well known. If someone recognized them, news would likely spread, and pilgrims would swarm to Kaihuang Temple.

Thus, the two concealed their forms and Qi, standing beside Master Duanchen.

Receiving pilgrims involved enlightening them and resolving their worries and troubles.

The first pilgrim was a delicately frail woman, weak as if a gust of wind could blow her over, weeping incessantly.

“Master, many years ago, my fiancé ran away with my maid on our wedding night. I tried to let it go, but after so many years, I simply cannot forget him.”

Master Duanchen smiled faintly, took out a Jade Vase, and filled it with water.

“Female benefactor, look. This Jade Vase is you. To let go of someone, you need to fill yourself with new things to replace that person.”

“Master, I have understood.”

“Initially, the water in the vase would gradually dry up, but you kept thinking about him, making the water in the vase increase instead of focusing on other things.”

The second pilgrim was a rather forthright woman, though she couldn’t help but reveal her sadness when discussing the past.

“Master, I had a childhood friend with whom I shared everything. Times were hard, and to make ends meet, my father sold me to the Yuan family to be a personal maid to the young mistress. Then one day, I saw the fiancé of the young mistress, who was my childhood friend. He took me away on the mistress’s wedding night, intending to wander the world with me, but shortly after our marriage, he said he wanted to renounce worldly attachments and join Buddhism. So many years have passed, and I still cannot forget him.”

Master Duanchen smiled faintly, took out a Jade Vase filled with water, and poured out the water.

“Female benefactor, have you understood?”

The second pilgrim seemed pensive: “Spilled water is hard to regain. My husband’s departure is an unchangeable reality, and I need to accept this fact and move forward. Master, I have understood.”

The third pilgrim was a wandering monk with a darkened face, seemingly from long periods of wandering.

“Master, my cultivation has reached a bottleneck.

“I had a fiancée and a childhood friend, both wonderful girls. I chose my childhood friend, and after getting married, suddenly one day, as I was with my childhood friend, I had an epiphany: familial and romantic love are emotions that bind me. I felt everything was meaningless; perhaps Buddhism was calling me. I left my childhood friend and became a wandering monk. Yet, after so many years, I feel increasingly unsuited for seclusion. Please, master, resolve my doubts.”

Master Duanchen smiled faintly and used magic skills to gather the water on the ground back into the Jade Vase.

The third pilgrim suddenly had a realization: “Master, does this mean there’s a chance to make amends? Can I return to secular life?”

Master Duanchen then hit the pilgrim over the head with the vase, knocking him out.

“I find it satisfying to hit people like this.”

Before losing consciousness, the pilgrim vaguely saw Master Duanchen flipping over a table and quickly opened the door to call out to the first two pilgrims who were about to leave: “Two female benefactors, hold off on letting go!”

Chapter 986: 985 Could My Brother Have Been Possessed

Master Duanchen gathered the three pilgrims in a side hall, letting them mutter about their own affairs, no longer concerning himself with them.

“I’ve caused the two benefactors some amusement,” Master Duanchen said with an apologetic smile, “usually, a poor monk like myself would not lift a hand in receiving pilgrims.”

“Master, you truly have a temperament that I admire,” Meng Jingzhou placed one hand over his chest, appreciating what Master Duanchen had done earlier.

Master Duanchen sighed, “Actually, there are not a few who are like the third pilgrim in recent years. I really don’t know what’s gotten into them.”

“They don’t know what evil has possessed them. Thinking that their wives, children, and families are bonds, they abandon their wives and children and enter the monastic life. After a few months or years, when they’ve come to their senses, they want to return to secular life. Not just remote temples, but even the few nearby ones, to my knowledge, have encountered similar situations.”

“Such things happen?” Lu Yang mused, feeling something was off about this matter.

“Fairy, do you see anything unusual about the third pilgrim?”

Immortal Fairy used her Immortal Sense to sweep the next side hall, “He seems alright, just a normal person, albeit one who’s been badly beaten but still dares not fight back.”

Lu Yang frowned. Was he overthinking it?

“Old Meng, what do you think?”

“The master struck well.”

“...”

The following pilgrims they received were much more normal, and after all, Master Duanchen was a high monk who had made a vast vow. He truly had real skills, and the two of them, Lu Yang, watched and learned a lot by the side.

Even though the method of guidance was just to take a bottle, fill it with water, and pour it out.

But one couldn’t withstand the manner of speaking and actions of Master Duanchen, which were all very appropriate, able to gradually pull the pilgrims into his own process.

“To make the other party believe in you, background, lighting, speech, behavior, and expressions... these are all very important,” Lu Yang continuously analyzed various aspects of Master Duanchen’s approach, looking to see in which areas he himself still fell short.

The last pilgrim of the day was somewhat unexpected for Lu Yang; it was the little girl who had nearly run into him the previous day. Though her clothes were patched, indicating a poor family background, both the clothes and her face were clean, which was endearing to the onlooker.

According to the little girl’s self-introduction, her name was Tang Qiaoqiao, fourteen years old. She had a brother, upon whom she depended, named Tang Chuanwu.

“Master, I don’t know what’s gotten into my brother recently. He seems to have become a completely different person, always suspicious and constantly practicing boxing. I don’t know where he learned this martial art. I asked my brother what happened, but he won’t tell me.”

Tang Qiaoqiao, thinking of the methods of demon cultivators described in the storytelling, with teary eyes and biting her lips hesitantly said, “Master, you don’t think my brother could have been possessed by someone, right?”

Master Duanchen activated the scriptures hanging on the wall to calm Tang Qiaoqiao down, showing a kind and convincing smile on his face.

“Little benefactor, your brother’s behavior shows that he is not a victim of possession. Those who are possessed would do their best to act normal to avoid raising suspicions. They wouldn’t change their temperament significantly.”

“Those capable of possessing others are usually cultivators who have achieved some level of cultivation. Such people are meticulous in their thinking and would certainly not expose any flaws. Even if someone familiar interacted with them, they wouldn’t notice any difference.”

Unlike Great Xia, which was extremely strict in management, in Buddha Country, one encountered those who had been possessed from time to time, even ones who diligently practiced cultivation after possessing someone and became abbots. Having encountered possessors several times, Master Duanchen knew the characteristics of such people.

Lu Yang nodded. What Master Duanchen said was right. When the Fairy had possessed him, she had also tried her best to mimic his actions, albeit the mimicry was hard to describe.

“So what’s going on with my brother?

Gripping the Buddha Beads, Master Duanchen pondered for a moment before saying, “Perhaps there is indeed some danger you’re unaware of. After learning about it through some channel, your brother, not wanting you to worry, has yet to tell you.”

Master Duanchen pulled a yellow talisman from his sleeve, wrapped it with a red string, and hung it around Tang Qiaoqiao’s neck, “Keep this talisman safe. Should you encounter danger, it can protect you thoroughly.”

“But I have no money,” Tang Qiaoqiao said, looking down at her toes as she spoke in a tiny voice, her anxiety palpable, fearful that Master Duanchen might throw her out.

“No matter, little Benefactor, if you perform more good deeds in the future, consider the talisman paid for.”

“Ah? Thank you, Master!” Tang Qiaoqiao abruptly looked up.

Master Duanchen brought his palms together and chanted the name of the Worldly Venerable: “A monk holds compassion at heart, I hope the little Benefactor won’t have a need for this talisman.”

To have no need for a talisman meant there would be no danger, which was naturally the best possible outcome.

After Kaihuang Temple was closed, Lu Yang followed Tang Qiaoqiao in secret, worried that if Tang Chuanwu had really been possessed, and the one who possessed him was as unschooled in subterfuge as the Fairy, then it would be disastrous.

The place where Tang Qiaoqiao lived was in a terrible environment, reeking of filth, houses crammed together, tortuous roads without any planning, built haphazardly, leaving nowhere for the sunlight to seep in.

“Brother, I’m back,” Tang Qiaoqiao said, in higher spirits than usual after Master Duanchen’s guidance. She had also bought some fish bones that could be used to make soup.

Tang Chuanwu, bare-chested, was drenched in sweat, his gaze determined, practicing his movements that seemed honed through thousands of repetitions, each one exceedingly precise.

“Brother, do we really have to train in martial arts so strenuously?” Tang Qiaoqiao asked tenderly.

Tang Chuanwu exhaled and affectionately patted his little sister’s head, “Brother needs to become stronger to protect you.”

“I am very safe,” she said.

Tang Chuanwu sighed, his eyes drifting to an unknown point, “It’s always no mistake to be prepared.”

“But I heard the masters at Kaihuang Temple say that they drink Decoction of Ten Complete Supplements daily to replenish their bodies during martial arts training. We can’t afford that. What if training hurts you?”

They were poor and could only afford to buy fish bones to nourish her brother, hoping they would help.

“It’s okay, brother will find a way to make money.”

From the shadows, Lu Yang raised an eyebrow, finding Tang Chuanwu’s movements familiar: “Long Arm Boxing?”

Only monks from the Kaihuang Temple should know this boxing technique. How did Tang Chuanwu know it? He didn’t seem like he had joined the Kaihuang Temple either.

Lu Yang was concerned that whoever had possessed Tang Chuanwu might be at a higher realm than himself, and recklessly using Spiritual Sense to probe might scare them off, so he asked Immortal Fairy to take action.

“He’s just a child, there are no signs of possession,” Immortal Fairy said.

Lu Yang touched the three Seeking Immortal Tokens in his possession, to no response.

“That’s good.”

If there was no possession, then it meant he had simply learned Long Arm Boxing clandestinely. Such minor matters were not worth his concern—as long as the person was fine.

Before leaving, Lu Yang hesitated, then secretly left a few Gold Beans at Tang Chuanwu’s house. Tang Qiaoqiao was right; if he continued to train like this, his body would indeed suffer and need strong medicinal soups to recover.

Seeing how precocious the two children were, they should know the principle of not flaunting wealth, and wouldn’t parade around with the Gold Beans.

Consider it a good deed done.

Before long, Tang Chuanwu discovered the Gold Beans on the table and couldn’t help feeling a chill down his spine: “Who’s there!”

Chapter 987: 986 Good People Are Rewarded

Tang Chuanwu could be certain that half an hour earlier, there were no Gold Beans on the table.

He became alert, having practiced martial arts for over half a year, his senses had improved, and even a Qi Refinement sixth layer cultivator would not be able to appear silently and place the Gold Beans on the table.

Who was it?

Friend or foe?

Previously, his younger sister had been discovered by the leader of the local snake, the Great Desert Gang, to possess a Spirit Root, intending to use her body to cultivate evil methods. They had brazenly sent over Silver, calling it “Life-purchasing Money”. When his sister refused, their subordinates, endowed with Cultivation Levels, went too far and killed her in a heavy-handed attack. He had rushed to her side, only to be killed as well.

Afterward, he had reincarnated several times, constantly gathering information, and learned two important pieces of news: the Deputy Gang Leader of the Great Desert Gang had long coveted the position of Gang Leader, and the Wolf-slaying Gang, the sworn enemy of the Great Desert Gang, had always been envious of their territory.

He then instigated a war between the two gangs. During the chaos, the Deputy Gang Leader killed the Gang Leader and became the new leader of the Great Desert Gang. No one knew of his sister’s Spirit Root, and this turned out to be what had helped his sister avoid the disaster.

Tang Chuanwu called out, “I don’t know which master has aided my sister and me, but please, master, leave a name so we can erect a stele in your honor to express our gratitude for your kindness.”

There was no response.

He waited for a moment, and then Tang Chuanwu called out again, but still, no response.

“Could it be Life-purchasing Money again?”

Tang Chuanwu could not discern the other party’s intentions, which made him incredibly uneasy.

“I really need to find out who the person leaving the Gold Beans is!”

He picked up the kitchen knife from the cutting board and plunged it into his heart!

...

After leaving the Gold Beans, Lu Yang returned to Kaihuang Temple.

“Old Lu, where did you go that has made you so happy?” Meng Jingzhou asked curiously. This youngster had been sneaking around after Qiaoqiao; why did he seem so cheerful now?

Lu Yang replied with a smile, humming a little tune, “I went to do a good deed. Doesn’t Buddhism advocate that good people receive good rewards? This is called performing acts of kindness...”

Before he finished speaking, Lu Yang’s vision darkened, and he heard the first worshipper complaining tearfully to Master Duanchen.

“Master, many years ago, my fiancé eloped with my maidservant on the night of our wedding...”

Lu Yang: “...”

Is this what they mean by good people receiving good rewards?

Why has time reversed again just like that?

Did the user of the Time Dao Fruit encounter something again?

Although Lu Yang knew that Master Duanchen would spend the whole day filling and pouring out water in a vase and what kind of worshippers he would encounter, he had already watched from behind Duanchen and now leaving seemed to suggest boredom, as if he didn’t care for watching anymore, which could be disrespectful to the master.

Lu Yang had no choice but to stay put, watching the events unfold behind him like a replay, without the ability to fast forward.

When the final worshipper, Tang Qiaoqiao, left, the entire day’s Recording Orb replay had finally come to an end.

Originally, Lu Yang thought of giving the Gold Beans directly to Tang Qiaoqiao, letting her take them home. However, he worried that the young girl might accidentally lose the money on her way home. It seemed more prudent to deliver it straight to her house.

This time, without needing Qiaoqiao to lead, Lu Yang headed straight for her house. By this time, Tang Chuanwu had finished a set of boxing, and was sitting next to the table resting and drinking water.

Lu Yang thought about it and decided to place the Gold Beans on the kitchen stove instead of the table, avoiding startling Tang Chuanwu with a sudden appearance and then he left quietly.

Tang Chuanwu waited until Tang Qiaoqiao returned home. The siblings chatted for half a day, and Tang Chuanwu kept an eye on the table, but from the beginning to the end, he never saw the Gold Beans appear.

“Strange, why haven’t they appeared yet?”

It wasn’t until Tang Qiaoqiao took a fishbone to the kitchen stove that she cried out in surprise, “Where did these Gold Beans come from?”

Tang Chuanwu felt a chill down his spine. He had deliberately sat by the table because he wanted to see how the other party would make their move.

The appearance of the Gold Beans on the kitchen stove meant the other party had seen him sitting by the table and opted to switch the location so as not to be discovered.

“Anyone capable of this must be at least a Foundation Establishment Great Master, and possibly even a Golden Core Ancestor.”

As Tang Chuanwu pondered, the way the Gold Beans were placed didn’t seem malevolent,

If it were an act involving financial transactions, it would have been hidden better and not found so easily, and if it were Life-purchasing Money, it would have been placed right in front of him.

“Could it be someone saw the plight of us siblings and bestowed them upon us out of pity?”

Perhaps the giver was a Golden Core Ancestor wandering the mortal world, acting casually; he was being overly nervous, viewing everything as a threat.

It’s no wonder Tang Chuanwu was cautious; he knew his modest strength was nothing in front of a Foundation Establishment Great Master and that he shouldn’t act too arrogantly simply because he had the ability to reverse time.

Reversing time was not invincible; it had its flaws.

After experiencing hundreds of active and passive reversals, he had come to understand the two main characteristics of this ability—it required his own death as a prerequisite, and he could only reverse to the hours of 7 a.m to 9 a.m, never exceeding a day.

If a Demon Cultivator discovered his oddity and conducted a Soul Search to learn about his experiences, then he would likely be in deep trouble.

Soul Searching wouldn’t kill him, only render him insane, turning him into a fool.

A Demon Cultivator could easily imprison the post-Soul Search him and simply kill him when he tried to reverse time, and after the reversal, he would still remain in a foolish state, becoming nothing but a tool for the Demon Cultivator.

He would have no chance to turn the tables.

...

“Benefactors, the Buddha Bathing Festival is in two months. May I ask if I could promote your presence?” Master Duanchen had been waiting outside Lu Yang and his companion’s guest room since early morning, anticipating their departure.

The invitation merely asked Lu Yang and his companion to visit Kaihuang Temple; it was Lu Yang’s guess that they were asked to help boost the attendance. Now it was confirmed that they were indeed invited to attract cultivators from other regions.

Meng Jingzhou patted his chest and gave a thumbs up, “Of course you can. My brother and I are upright people; the Master can promote us as he pleases.”

“Then I, the humble monk, thank both benefactors in advance.”

“I believe that to gain fame quickly, one must rely on ‘events’.”

Under the management of Master Duanchen, Kaihuang Temple’s incense offerings were becoming more and more prosperous; he had his own set of tried and tested promotional tactics.

“For example, our Kaihuang Temple has a reputation on par with several other temples nearby; pilgrims usually choose whichever temple is closer, with no real differentiation.”

“To address this, the humble monk has captured many evil spirits and poison creatures, placing them not far from Kaihuang City, pretending they are about to launch a major attack on the city. Then I step in and deal with them all, naturally surpassing the reputation of the other temples.”

“I’d like to ask you both to pretend to encounter an evil spirit in the city and eliminate it. In this way, your arrival is tied to an event, and the common folk, who love discussing such matters over meals, will likely spread the news of your presence in Kaihuang City very quickly.”

After some thought, Lu Yang and his companion agreed.

Chapter 988: 987: Accidentally Encountering an Evil Spirit

"In the city, there resides a family of Goodman Cui, who, according to descriptions, have a three-eyed ghost child not yet mature in its powers. This ghost child, possessing only a mild Yin Energy, can only roam during the night to scare people for fun, causing the family of Goodman Cui much distress. The incident has become well-known in the vicinity and has had a significant impact,"

"This ghost child has some intelligence, as whenever monks come to exorcise it, it hides and leaves no trace."

"I wonder if I might trouble both benefactors to go and eliminate this ghost child?"

Master Duanchen originally planned to send a disciple tonight, as the three-eyed ghost child is not a formidable ghost object, and any Qi Cultivation Stage cultivator holding a magic artifact could handle it.

The only difficulty lies in locating it.

Now happens to be a good opportunity to request Benefactor Lu and Benefactor Meng to go.

"This matter is not difficult."

"Also, when you two eliminate the three-eyed ghost child, could you make a bit of a show? The local people, not understanding magic skills, believe that only those with impressive displays are powerful," Master Duanchen reminded.

"That too is not difficult."

...

"Excuse me, is this the residence of Goodman Cui?"

Upon opening the door, the servant looked baffled at the two individuals who did not appear to be from Buddha Country, "Who are you?"

"My name is Lu Jingzhou, and this is my Junior Brother Meng Yang. We happened to pass by and heard about the ghost haunting the residence of Goodman Cui. We wish to stay overnight to eliminate the ghost,"

The servant was delighted upon hearing this, as it would be wonderful if the two could eliminate the ghost; he had been scared to sleep at night.

"Let me go and inform the master of the house," the servant ran off to inform Goodman Cui.

Goodman Cui was a portly middle-aged man, overjoyed at the news that cultivators were willing to come and remove the ghost.

After verifying the two men’s forged identity credentials, Goodman Cui cordially invited them into the Cui residence.

"Masters, please make sure to eliminate the ghost. Our family is so frightened that we can hardly sleep at night."

"We shall certainly do our best," Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou replied as they sat upright, drinking tea, each sitting on only one-third of their chair, exuding the demeanor of experts.

"Dad, look what I bought," a young girl ran cheerily into the hall. Upon seeing there were guests, she slowed to a walk and sheepishly asked, "Who are these two?"

Goodman Cui glared at the girl but quickly turned to introduce with a smile, "These two are cultivators from the Great Xia Wandering Monks Sect. This is Master Lu Jingzhou and Master Meng Yang, both Great Cultivators at the Half-Step Golden Core Stage."

"Masters, this is my daughter, Cui Que’er,"

"Pleased to meet you, Miss Cui,"

"From Great Xia?" Cui Que’er blinked at the two men, pulling a lantern out from behind her.

"Then you must recognize this, right?"

"Is that a solar-powered lamp?" Lu Yang thought to himself, could I not recognize it when I have a stake in it?

"Yeah, I just bought it from a merchant. It’s said to light up without any need for lighting,"

Lu Yang nodded, showing a clean and young knightly smile, "Indeed, this is quite popular in Great Xia. Even in Demon Territory, many of the Demon Race use it, and candles have become rather rare,"

"Dad, I’ll go back first then,"

Eager to try out this wondrous lamp, Cui Que’er hurriedly left.

"I apologize for that,"

"Not at all, Goodman Cui. It’s a good thing for Miss Cui to be playful,"

"By the way, since hearsay isn’t very accurate, could Goodman Cui elaborate on this matter with the ghost, so that we can better prepare ourselves,"

Upon speaking of the ghost’s troubling, Goodman Cui sighed incessantly.

"There has been a ghost in my house, and I don’t even know how long it’s been around. Half a month ago, in the middle of the night, I got up to relieve myself. The room was pitch dark, illuminated by just a sliver of moonlight."

"By chance, I glanced at the bronze mirror hanging on the wall and noticed that it reflected the figures of two people!"

"I jerked my head around to look, but I didn’t see anything. When I looked back at the mirror, the other figure had also disappeared."

"At the time, I just thought it was an illusion and didn’t think much of it."

"But then, something strange happened to my wife as well. Ten days ago, she couldn’t sleep and thought to go sit in the courtyard. When she got there, she suddenly saw a person crouching by the wall, stretching their neck to look at her, and that person had three eyes."

"But the strangest thing was that our home’s wall is three meters high!"

"My wife screamed, and everyone came outside, but the person suddenly disappeared by the wall. When the servant climbed the wall with a ladder to check, he found two large black handprints on the edge of the wall."

"Since then, our family often sees evil spirits at night, and we’re so scared that even if we wake up, we dare not open our eyes, fearing we might see something we shouldn’t. We beg you two masters to help save our family from this peril!"

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou exchanged glances. According to records, the Three-eyed Ghost Child often appeared in mirrors and on wall edges, liked to morph into various forms to scare people, and fed on fear. Everything matched.

"Goodman Cui, rest assured, both of us brothers are skilled in removing demons. Tonight, we will definitely eradicate this evil spirit and bring peace to your home!"

Goodman Cui was moved and stood up to bow deeply, "Yours Truly Cui thanks the two masters!"

Lu Yang helped Goodman Cui to his feet, "At midnight tonight, we will perform the ritual in the courtyard. Please clear a space for us."

"I will have someone tidy up the courtyard right away."

Goodman Cui quickly ordered his servant to clean up the courtyard, and soon it was neat and tidy.

Then Goodman Cui also hosted a banquet to honor the two masters, who were Great Cultivators at the Half-step Golden Core Stage. What evil spirit could not be easily subdued by them?

As night fell and midnight approached, Goodman Cui waited anxiously for the result in his room.

In the courtyard, Lu Yang arranged swords according to the Postnatal Eight Diagrams. These were ordinary Spirit Swords, used to set up the Langhuan Sword Formation. This formation was not very powerful, especially compared to the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array, but it was quite spectacular when activated.

Meng Jingzhou, meanwhile, pretended to perform rituals in the courtyard but was actually releasing a strong Qi and Blood power.

"Rise!"

Under the night sky, Lu Yang shouted, and the Sword Array activated, transforming into a spread of blue light that enveloped the entire Cui Family. Meng Jingzhou’s Qi and Blood power appeared as red, drifting towards the sky in strands.

The fusion of blue and red ravaged the scene, illuminating the Cui Family as bright as day.

"Ah, such painful Sword Qi!"

The Three-eyed Monk burst forth from the earth, glaring menacingly at the Sword Array, "Who are these rogue cultivators daring to meddle in my affairs!"

The monk had an exceedingly long neck and radiated an ominous aura, looking exceedingly eerie.

"Who has such vigorous Qi and Blood!"

The shadow on the ground wriggled and transformed into a pitch-black figure, only the eyes hollow.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou looked at the Three-eyed Monk and the Hollow Shadow, a bit dumbfounded.

Who are you guys, where is the promised Three-eyed Ghost Child?

Cui Que’er, hearing the noise outside, rubbed her eyes wanting to see what was happening.

But unbeknownst to her, a child with three eyes floated in front of her, nearly touching her face, and showed a creepy smile, just waiting for Cui Que’er to open her eyes!

"Where’s the light?" Cui Que’er fumbled for the solar lamp and finally found the switch.

Click.

The solar lamp lit up, and the Three-eyed Child screamed, turning into ashes.

Cui Que’er forced her eyes open and saw nothing, slightly puzzled, "Why do I feel like I heard a scream?"

Chapter 989: 988: Are all the Three-eyed Ghost Children in Buddha Country this big?

Master Duanchen, standing not far away, gazed blankly at the two malevolent spirits that had appeared above Goodman Cui’s home, then looked down at the one he had brought in a birdcage, which was shivering with fear, a rare expression of bewilderment on his face.

His original plan had been, should Lu Yang and his companion kill the Three-eyed Ghost Child too quickly, to bring over a malevolent spirit and continue the killing spree, aiming to cause a larger disturbance.

"Where did these two come from?"

Now it seemed there was no need for his preparation, as the malevolent spirits at Goodman Cui’s house were much stronger than the one he had brought.

Master Duanchen picked up the malevolent spirit, speaking calmly and peacefully, "As one who has renounced worldly ties, compassion should be my abode. I can’t be bothered to take you back, just kill yourself."

The malevolent spirit understood human speech and shook its head vigorously.

Seeing this, Master Duanchen sighed gently, "Since you are unwilling to kill yourself, then I can only help you."

After saying this, Master Duanchen chanted scriptures and released the light of Buddha, which directly incinerated the imprisoned malevolent spirit in the birdcage.

...

The Three-eyed Ghost Monk glared viciously at Lu Yang with all three eyes, "Who would have thought that after hiding in Kaihuang City for over fifty years, I would be discovered by you today, you all deserve to die!"

"Just two juniors at the half-step to Golden Core, do you really think you can defy the heavens!"

The Hollow Shadow glared at Meng Jingzhou, its resentment overwhelming, "The power of Qi and Blood reminds me of the old monk who doomed me to become a fierce ghost, such a repugnant feeling!"

The Three-eyed Ghost Monk and the Hollow Shadow, both Nascent Soul Peak Ghost Cultivators, joined forces, utilizing forbidden secret techniques in addition to various magic artifacts, Talismans, Array Methods, and then, stirred by Elixirs that unlocked their potential and backed by the reinforcement of Cultivation Techniques at night, they could just manage to match the power of a cultivator in the initial stage of Spirit Transformation.

Otherwise, they wouldn’t dare to show themselves so brazenly in Kaihuang City; they were confident they could escape from Master Duanchen’s hands.

Lu Yang slowly drew his Green Peak Sword, the moonlight reflecting off the blade and illuminating his furrowed brows with a brilliant shine.

Facing such formidable enemies, Lu Yang dared not hold back, "Even though I knew Goodman Cui was haunted by a malevolent spirit, I didn’t expect them to be two Great Powers on the verge of Body Integration."

Meng Jingzhou took the fighting stance of Arhat Fist, ready to launch an attack at any moment, "Three-eyed Ghost Monk, could it be that you’ve cultivated the ’Three Talents Heavenly Eye’? And this Hollow Shadow, unfathomable, must have been cultivating a world-defying Divine Ability and went mad from walking the ’Incorporeal Path’."

"Tough opponents indeed."

The Three-eyed Ghost Monk and the Hollow Shadow were somewhat muddled by these words. What "Three Talents Heavenly Eye," what "Incorporeal Path"? If we could practice such outlandish Divine Abilities, would we still be stuck at the Nascent Soul Peak, unable to break through, and then have to lurk in Kaihuang City for over fifty years?

Lu Yang no longer concealed his Cultivation Level, his aura erupting like a volcano, the space around him twisting and barely forming the shape of mountains.

Meng Jingzhou’s Qi and Blood turned into a great dragon, while his Pure Yang Power shone like a brilliant sun, illuminating the entirety of Kaihuang Temple.

The Three-eyed Ghost Monk and the Hollow Shadow, feeling the aura from Lu Yang and his companion, experienced a drastic change in expression. They had surpassed the scope of their senses.

"You’ve been hiding your Cultivation Levels! Who are you!"

"Wait, there may be some misunderstanding between us..."

"Today, even if it costs us our lives, we, two brothers, must stop you!" Lu Yang, embracing his fate, raised the Green Peak Sword, the Sword Qi soaring to the sky and the sound of the sword’s hum unceasing, frightening the Three-eyed Ghost Monk, whose already pale face turned even paler without a trace of blood.

We really aren’t at the realm of Body Integration.

"Sword Sever Eternal Ages!" Lu Yang shouted loudly, his hair ties snapping as hair fluttered in the air, one sword strike as if it were cleaving through the river of time, freezing the moment, an ordinary strike filled with his full force.

"Overlord’s Sky Punch!" Qi and Blood formed a dragon twined around Meng Jingzhou’s fists, veins popping, clothes bursting open, eyes bloodshot, his cry echoing heaven and earth, an ordinary punch thrown as if it were the last of his life.

Boom, boom, boom—

The Green Peak Sword fell, cleaving the Three-eyed Ghost Monk in half from the middle, its cross-section sprouted countless tentacles, which put its body back together again.

Meng Jingzhou’s body shot out like a Profound Arrow, breaking through layers of sound barriers. He threw a punch that tore a large hole in the gut of the Hollow Shadow, one that could not heal.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou engaged in a one-sided battle, various Sword Qi and Qi and Blood swirling in a dazzling array, painting the sky with a riot of colors. The townspeople came out to watch but couldn’t see anything or make out who was fighting in the air.

Cui Que’er pushed open the window and looked up at the sky, noticing the kaleidoscopic movement and occasionally hearing the cries of Fierce Ghosts, terrified, she murmured, "Is this the evil spirit hiding in our house? How scary."

No one could see that the Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow floating in the air didn’t dare to move; any slight movement would cause them to be hit by the attacks, leading to their death and the destruction of their paths.

As for them being able to rival the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation through various means, facing such attacks, there wasn’t much difference between the Nascent Soul Peak and the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, having watched enough, shouted together. Their moves collided, causing a huge explosion. Taking advantage of the blinding explosion which made it hard to open one’s eyes, they swiftly grabbed the Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow and handed them over to Master Duanchen, who stood next to the Cui Family estate.

Then they returned to the center of the explosion, feigning severe injuries, spitting out two mouthfuls of blood. They fell from the sky, landing powerlessly in the center of the courtyard.

"Two masters!"

Goodman Cui, seeing this, ran over heedless of the danger, trying to lift the two.

Lu Yang, lying on the ground, gestured with his hand, his breath was frail—inhaling with no exhale—and yet he mustered his body to say in a weak voice, "We have not dishonored our mission, those two have been dealt with by us brothers."

"Goodman, do not panic, we are just tired and want to take a rest ..."

"Masters!"

An elderly monk’s figure appeared in the courtyard, squatting down to carefully examine Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou’s injuries.

"Master Duanchen," Goodman Cui bowed, guessing that the battle had attracted Master Duanchen.

"Master Duanchen, are these two masters in trouble?"

Master Duanchen brought his palms together in prayer, "The two benefactors are not in great distress, after the poor monk takes them to the temple, they should recover."

"That’s a relief," Goodman Cui exhaled a huge sigh of relief.

Master Duanchen carried the two back to Kaihuang Temple, holding two bird cages in his hand which contained the Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow.

Master Duanchen lived up to his monastic integrity, never speaking falsely. After bringing Lu Yang and the other to the temple, they immediately bounced back to life.

For tonight, their mission was complete. What remained was to wait for Kaihuang Temple’s propaganda.

"Finally, it’s resolved."

"But speaking of which, are the Three-eyed Ghost Children from your Buddha Country always this big?" Lu Yang carefully examined the sealed Three-eyed Ghost Monk. This didn’t match the descriptions in the books. The books said Three-eyed Ghost Children were only knee-high, but the captured ghost monk was half a head taller than himself. Could this thing evolve?

"Why were you frightening Goodman Cui’s family?" Meng Jingzhou asked.

At Nascent Soul Peak, they shouldn’t need to feed on fear. Besides, scaring Goodman Cui’s family every day wouldn’t fill them up, would it?

The Three-eyed Ghost Monk angrily retorted, "Who is scaring Goodman Cui? The two of us were just discussing meeting underground tonight, we have never been to the Cui Family before!"

Lu Yang let out a light "huh," puzzled, "Then where did the Three-eyed Ghost Child go?"

Chapter 990: 989: Fast Information Delivery

"Fairy, do you know where the Three-eyed Ghost Child went?" Lu Yang asked. They were supposed to stage a play with a little ghost, but even after the play was over, and they’d captured two Nascent Soul old ghosts, there was no sign of the Three-eyed Ghost Child.

"Just before you started fighting, the Cui Family’s little girl turned on a light, which burned the little ghost to death."

"Ah?"

Lu Yang realized it was the Sun Power from the solar lamp that killed the Three-eyed Ghost Child.

"I heard from the Yaoyang Sect that their lamps have some special abilities."

"A small fry like the Three-eyed Ghost Child, only daring to appear at night and never during the day, can’t withstand the exposure to a solar lamp."

"According to Master Duanchen, there are quite a few evil spirits here in Buddha Country, it seems solar lamps could sell well here."

Lu Yang estimated that his share of Spirit Stones from the Yaoyang Sect would increase again.

"I don’t even know how many Spirit Stones I have now."

Lu Yang had income from Flying Car royalties, a share from the Jiuyou Sect’s grill shop, and a share of the Yaoyang Sect’s solar products; he would occasionally receive a large sum of Spirit Stones, which he typically just accepted in full, too lazy to count.

Regardless, he couldn’t use them all up.

"Forget it, as long as it’s enough."

"You two benefactors rest. I can handle the interrogation of these two evil spirits myself," Master Duanchen said, smilingly looking at the Three-eyed Ghost Monk and the Hollow Shadow. According to these two ghosts, they’d been lurking in Kaihuang City for over fifty years, plotting who knows what.

He had worked hard managing Kaihuang City, and it had nearly been destroyed by these two ghosts.

Fortunately for him, the two benefactors helped him root out the trouble, and Master Duanchen was extremely relieved.

"Then we’ll leave it to the master," said Lu Yang. They left the matters of Kaihuang City to Master Duanchen without interfering.

The next day, news that Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou had arrived in Kaihuang City to help Goodman Cui rid his family of evil spirits spread throughout the alleys and streets.

In the teahouse, a fellow mysteriously told his friends, "Do you guys know what happened in last night’s battle?"

His friend, intrigued, quickly asked, "What happened, tell me quickly."

Upon hearing this, others perked up their ears to eavesdrop, and the noise in the teahouse lowered significantly.

"I have a friend at the Kaihuang Temple, he said that last night’s big battle involved two cultivators, Sword Immortal Lu and Buddhist Meng!"

"Is it that Sword Immortal Lu who went to the Fairy Palace and after which many people in the palace ended up pregnant?"

"Who else but that Sword Immortal Lu could it be?"

"Sheesh, I’m envious."

"Last night’s enemy was formidable as well, according to Sword Immortal Lu and Buddhist Meng. These enemies were Great Cultivators in the Body Integration Realm, lurking in Goodman Cui’s house, unbeknownst what their purpose was. They originally wanted to remain anonymous and just help Goodman Cui solve his family’s evil spirit problem and then leave, but they ended up uncovering such big enemies."

"They also said that they entered last night’s battle with a mindset of perishing together, and fortunately, they narrowly won with one move, defeating those two," people nearby couldn’t help but exclaim in admiration, "Divinity Transformation Stage challenging the Body Integration Realm?! How impressive?"

Realizing he had spoken too much, he quickly covered his mouth, pretending he had said nothing.

The man continued, "Last night, after Master Duanchen brought the two back to the temple, he took great care of them. They’re now out of danger. They also said they’d stay in Kaihuang City for a few more days to attend the upcoming Buddha Bathing Festival."

"That’s good news," his companion rejoiced.

After leaving the tea shop, the man, his companion, and others gathered secretly, removed their fake hair, exposing their shiny bald heads.

As Master Duanchen instructed, they’d completed their mission, now all left was to wait for the news to ferment.

"We didn’t lie just now, did we?"

"No, all the lines were prepared by the master, every word true."

"Were they prepared by the master, or did I hear that Benefactor Lu came up with them?"

Monks have many restrictions, and not lying is a major one. But now that Kaihuang Temple had guidance from a higher-up, it was no longer an issue.

Just as Master Duanchen had anticipated, the news of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou’s demon-removing feats not only spread quickly through Kaihuang City but also reached the surrounding cities in a very short time.

"Sword Immortal Lu and Buddhist Meng are attending the Buddha Bathing Festival in Kaihuang City?"

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were welcomed by both common folk and cultivators. Common folk wanted to see the legendary characters they often heard about in storytelling, while cultivators wanted to attend the Buddha Bathing Festival and receive guidance from Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

"Both are geniuses of this era, standing shoulder to shoulder with those in the Body Integration Realm."

"More than shoulder to shoulder, didn’t you hear that last night they took care of two Integration Ghost Cultivators?"

"Utterly terrifying."

For ordinary cultivators, merely receiving one or two pieces of advice from the duo of Lu Yang was a tremendous fortune.

"Brother Zou, are you going to attend the Buddha Bathing Festival in Kaihuang City?"

"Certainly. However, given the rare opportunity, I’m afraid the number of cultivators heading to Kaihuang City will exceed previous times."

...

Master Duanchen found Lu Yang and his companion.

"Master, what’s the matter?" Lu Yang wondered, seeing the master’s troubled expression, "Is it about insufficient publicity?"

"That’s not it. The results of the publicity were unexpectedly good; it’s the results from the Soul Search of the two ghosts."

"What about it?" Meng Jingzhou asked, noting that the problem must have been with the results of the Soul Search.

"These two had some ties to the Ruthless Sect, but they weren’t members. Rather, they were pawns of the Ruthless Sect."

"The Ruthless Sect promised them Soul Condensing Pills as a reward to stay in this city and carry out tasks for the sect."

"For example, the numerous individuals who left their families to become monks in recent years, this was also the doings of the Ruthless Sect."

"The Ruthless Sect developed a type of elixir called the Oblivion Pill, which dissolves up meeting water. Taking it can cause someone to temporarily lose their emotions for several years. Devoid of emotions or integrity, affected individuals often misconstrue this change as a spiritual awakening; once the effect wears off, and their awakened state dissipates, they desire to return to secular life."

"These two ghosts secretly administered the drug to people and observed its effects."

"I see." Lu Yang recalled asking Immortal Fairy to check if the third visitor had any abnormalities, likely by then the effect of the pill had worn off, revealing no oddities.

"Do we know who their superior is?" Meng Jingzhou asked, seizing the opportunity from last night’s event.

Master Duanchen shook his head, "We only know it’s an Integration Elder from the Ruthless Sect, but they have never seen his true face. He only contacted them directly. After last night’s incident, I doubt this elder will contact them again."

"The monk has also learned from their memories that there are others like them located across the cities in the eastern region of Buddha Country. I suspect that the Ruthless Sect has a significant branch within Buddha Country."

"Such a significant issue." Lu Yang frowned, no wonder Master Duanchen looked worried; this was not a trivial matter.

"I shall report this matter to Western Paradise Temple, but I hope you, Benefactors, could write a letter. Perhaps inform Great Xia as well. Maybe both can join forces to eradicate the Ruthless Sect’s influence in Buddha Country?"

Lu Yang thought for a moment, then nodded in agreement, "It can be done."

"Amitabha."

Seeing Lu Yang consenting, Master Duanchen felt a weight lifted off his shoulders and left the guest room feeling much relieved.

"How will you handle it, write a letter to the sect?" Meng Jingzhou asked, conscious that mailing a letter to Seek Dao Sect would take at least half a month, and there might be mishaps causing the letter to be lost along the way.

"No need for that trouble, I have a way."

"What way?"

Without answering, Lu Yang lay back on the bed under Meng Jingzhou’s puzzled gaze and became still, as if lifeless.

...

Dai Bufan yawned, lying comfortably inside a water sphere, relishing the thought of sleep.

Suddenly, Junior Brother Gu Junye burst in, panic-stricken, "Senior Brother, it’s terrible! Elder Brother Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp has extinguished."

"What?"

Hearing this, Dai Bufan choked on a mouthful of water, then hastily rushed to the Soul Lamp Chamber to check on Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp.

Upon arrival at the Soul Lamp Chamber, rows of Soul Lamps were lit, making the chamber bright, but only Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp had gone out.

Suddenly, Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp flickered back on, then went out again and lit up once more, reminiscent of a malfunctioning solar-powered lamp.

Dai Bufan’s expression turned increasingly peculiar; he remembered Lu Yang telling him that if a Soul Lamp followed a particular pattern of flickering, it could form words and convey messages.

Although he was prepared, seeing Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp flickering on and off still felt eerie.

Chapter 991: 990: Oblivion Pill

Dai Bufan took out a piece of paper and, according to the information recorded on it, carefully compared the messages conveyed by the flickering of the Soul Lamp, thus deciphering the intelligence that Lu Yang had transmitted with his life, his eyes twitching.

Dai Bufan had been coordinating intelligence in the Task Hall for years, analyzing which pieces of information hid the involvement of the Ruthless Sect. Unfortunately, even when he pinpointed the whereabouts of the Ruthless Sect, their cautious actions always led to empty pursuits upon arrival.

"I only went to Buddha Country a few days ago. How did I manage to catch the tail of the Ruthless Sect?"

Why did his careful analysis of the intelligence fail to locate the Ruthless Sect?

"Senior Brother, what is written on this paper?" Gu Junye asked curiously.

Dai Bufan folded the paper and explained, "Your Elder Brother Lu Yang invented this method of transmitting intelligence. Depending on the different durations of brightness of the Soul Lamp, it forms something called ’pinyin’, and when connected, it forms a sentence."

"The matter of the Ruthless Sect is of great importance and also involves Buddha Country. We must still let Eldest Senior Sister make the decision," Dai Bufan decided to consult Eldest Senior Sister.

"Junior Brother Gu, you continue to watch in the Soul Lamp Chamber. Let me know if there are any changes."

Dai Bufan arranged for Gu Junye to stay in the Soul Lamp Chamber, hoping he would stay put instead of stirring up trouble all day. Several people from the Dynasty had already used connections to inquire about him, eager to know which Great Power Gu Junye really was.

Dai Bufan repeatedly explained that Gu Junye was not a Great Power possessing a new body. This only made those people more convinced of their speculation, leading to quite a few marriage proposals.

...

Buddha Country, Kaihuang Temple, guest room.

Lu Yang came back to life, feeling a bit muddled, as using the Playing Dead Technique repeatedly had taken a significant toll on his spiritual power.

"Has the message been sent back to the sect?" Meng Jingzhou asked, "What is this magic skill?"

Lu Yang smiled proudly, "This was taught by an Immortal, integrating the causality, the doctrine of life and death from ancient times, the great divine skill known as ’Deceiving Heaven’."

"Is it that powerful?" Meng Jingzhou was astounded. Although he had never heard of such a divine ability from ancient times, the name alone indicated its terrifying strength.

It’s possible that due to the overwhelming power of this magic skill, it was deemed a forbidden practice and thus lost to time.

The two went to the Abbot’s Room to find Master Duanchen.

"Master, we have already informed the sect of the situation."

"Ah? Already?" Master Duanchen looked down at the letter he had only half-written.

He hadn’t even finished the letter, and sending it to the Western Paradise Temple would take over ten days.

"We have a secret method of communication."

Master Duanchen thought to himself, truly fitting for disciples of the Immortal Sect. His own modest background could not compare.

"What brings you to this humble monk?"

"Just now, master mentioned that Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow drugged people with an Oblivion Pill. We would both like to see this Oblivion Pill," they said.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, hearing for the first time about the Elixir that stripped people of their emotions, were quite curious.

"This humble monk has sent people to the stronghold of the two ghosts. There should still be some Oblivion Pills left there. How about you wait until this humble monk finishes this letter, and then accompany you both to the stronghold?"

"We would be troubled, but thank you, Master."

...

"Both the Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow are extremely cautious ghosts. Concerned about the Ruthless Sect’s tasks assigned to them being exposed, they haven’t recruited any followers but have personally completed every task," Master Duanchen shared further intelligence about the two ghosts as they headed to the stronghold.

"This is the place." Master Duanchen led the two to a river, known as the Canal, which spanned through Kaihuang City, providing water to all its residents.

The canal water was crystal clear, a result of the dedicated maintenance by Kaihuang Temple.

After Master Duanchen excavated the river, it soon developed problems with people dumping garbage and other items into it, making the water filthy and diseased to drink. Master Duanchen then had no choice but to assign people to maintain the water.

"Their base is at the bottom of the canal."

The three of them jumped into the river, swam to the bottom, and found a hollow in the riverbed. It turned out that the monks from Kaihuang Temple had cleared away the sediment and silt and dug several meters deeper, exposing the base below.

There was a thin, transparent film of water between the canal and the base, preventing the river water from flooding the base.

The three of them passed through the water membrane and entered the base.

Inside the base, monks were busy tallying captured supplies, registering and storing spirit stones box by box into storage rings.

Master Duanchen called over the leading monk and said a few words to him, after which the leading monk brought over several bottles of elixirs.

"This is the Oblivion Pill," Master Duanchen poured out a few white pills, which looked quite ordinary.

"Based on the soul search results of the two ghosts, taking one pill can cause a person to lose their emotions for three to five years. That’s the effect on mortals; for cultivators, the higher the cultivation level, the shorter the duration of lost emotions."

"That miraculous?"

Lu Yang nodded and analyzed, "Previously, when we soul-searched the high-ranking members of the Ruthless Sect, we didn’t find anything like the Oblivion Pill, which seems to indicate that the Oblivion Pill must have been developed only in recent years; otherwise, they wouldn’t be looking everywhere to experiment with it."

Having said that, Lu Yang swallowed the Oblivion Pill directly.

After swallowing the Oblivion Pill, Lu Yang felt as if everything in the world lost its meaning, yet his mind was clearer than ever before.

After a few moments, the effects of the drug passed, and emotions surged back like a tide, returning Lu Yang to normal.

"No wonder so many people took the pill and then became monks; it really is somewhat interesting."

Meng Jingzhou also took a pill, his pupils constricted sharply, and his body shook violently, feeling an unprecedented inner peace and excellent state.

A few moments later, the effects of the drug passed.

"Why was your reaction so strong?" Lu Yang wondered.

Meng Jingzhou couldn’t suppress the smile on his lips as he looked at the Oblivion Pill like it was a treasure, "This is good stuff. Ever since the Sun Power increased when the Sun seal was broken, my Single Body Spiritual Root has been restless, and I’ve had to divert part of my spiritual power to suppress the evil fire."

Meng Jingzhou grew more excited as he spoke, "Just now, after taking the pill, the evil fire disappeared, and my spiritual power was highly concentrated. My body feels incredibly good!"

He felt that after taking the Oblivion Pill, his combat power had risen to a new level.

Lu Yang thought about it, and realized Meng Jingzhou’s condition was actually understandable, "Wilted?"

Meng Jingzhou was furious, "You’re the one who’s wilted... wait, it seems like this is the state described in books after a passionate night?"

"Regardless of what you say, this definitely is a good thing."

Meng Jingzhou checked his bodily condition and found no side effects.

"The Ruthless Sect still knows how to do human things, huh."

"This Oblivion Pill really is a good thing," Lu Yang nodded, not mentioning the special bonus it gave to Old Meng, but recognizing its practical value alone was very high.

"Since one pill can make mortals forget emotions for three to five years, wouldn’t scraping off some powder enable mortals to forget their emotions for a few hours?"

"In an Oblivion state, spiritual power is highly concentrated, and thinking is more active than usual. If it were used in examinations like the imperial exams, it would prevent anxiety from not being able to solve problems, and both the efficiency and results of problem-solving would greatly improve."

"It should also be effective for treating ailments like heartbreak."

Chapter 992: 911: Meng Jingzhou Becoming Increasingly Destined with Buddha Country

Lu Yang thought more and more that the value of the Oblivion Pill was immeasurable. It was an excellent elixir that could concentrate attention, improve efficiency in handling affairs, and even treat spiritual illnesses; moreover, if a cultivator took it, it could help them enter a meditative trance quickly.

"By the way, Master, do you know why the Ruthless Sect wanted to develop the Oblivion Pill?"

"It seems to be because the higher-ups of the Ruthless Sect in our Buddha Country accidentally came into contact with Benefactor Lu’s Second Senior Brother. They were so infuriated by Benefactor Lu’s Second Senior Brother that their cultivation was disrupted and they needed the Oblivion Pill to calm their mind state."

"What a waste of heavenly materials, just for that?"

Lu Yang shook his head. The higher-ups of the Ruthless Sect were not maintaining their mind state properly. First his master, and now his Second Senior Brother, how could just about anyone disrupt their mind state?

"Does the Three-eyed Ghost Monk have the formula for the Oblivion Pill?"

Master Duanchen shook his head, "I don’t know. From their memories, it seems that the formula of the Oblivion Pill is a secret of the Ruthless Sect. I’m afraid even the elders of the Ruthless Sect who assigned them their tasks do not know the formula."

Lu Yang sighed. It was a pity. He had intended to grab the formula for the Oblivion Pill, tap into its potential, and make money for the Ruthless Sect, but they didn’t give him the opportunity. They truly did not show face to this Young Sect Master.

"Although I do not know the formula, I know what herbs are needed to refine the Oblivion Pill."

Lu Yang was quite surprised, "Fairy, you have this skill too?"

Immortal Fairy crossed her arms, reveling in Lu Yang’s surprised expression, her head tilting proudly toward the sky: "Of course. To create an unparalleled fist technique, I have done extensive research on the properties of herbs."

Lu Yang remembered, the Immortal Fist Technique is derived from Health Preservation Boxing, which includes techniques like Arhat Fruit Fist, Croton Fist, Hundred Grass Withering Fist, etc. If one did not study the properties of herbs, one could indeed not invent it.

"Let me see, this Oblivion Pill contains licorice, Three Spirit Flower, Bodhi Leaf..."

Immortal Fairy listed over a hundred different herbs in one breath, accurately stating their proportions.

"I can’t discern the pill concoction technique," said Immortal Fairy, who was not skilled in refining pills and had little research in this area.

"But refining pills is like cooking in principle. You throw stones, herbs, and whatnot into a pot and then cook them fiercely. I can research the ingredients of the Oblivion Pill and stir-fry them into a dish; the effects should be the same. Oh no, they will definitely be better."

"Although I don’t know how to make Oblivion Pill, I can make Forgetfulness Meal. After they ate one plate of it, the Yingtian Immortals went to sleep, woke up forgetting whether they had eaten, so I made them another plate. When they had bad memory, they could eat more than a dozen plates."

"Forgetfulness and memory loss are probably interconnected, and with my skills, I should be able to work it out quickly by analogy."

"That’s enough," Lu Yang broke into a cold sweat, quickly stopping Immortal Fairy’s idea. That much help was enough; any more would be dangerous.

When he captures the higher-up of the Ruthless Sect who was infuriated by his Second Senior Brother, he might as well feed him the Forgetfulness Meal. It wouldn’t be a loss to treat him well with an executioner’s last meal.

An Immortal cooking, how many people seek such an incredible opportunity in vain.

Although the elixir formula was still unknown, learning about the herbs used in the Oblivion Pill was indeed a good thing.

To refine elixirs, the Ruthless Sect will undoubtedly purchase or cultivate these herbs on a large scale. Perhaps this information could help Elder Brother Dai follow this clue and find the traces of the Ruthless Sect.

But with over a hundred herbs, how many times would one have to die to pass on the message?

...

Western Paradise Temple.

The ruler of Buddha’s Country, Master Mingyu of Western Paradise Temple, held his Buddha beads and regarded the visitor with calm eyes, keeping him at arm’s length.

"Benefactor Jiang Qun, our temple is aware of the matters concerning the Ruthless Sect. We will dispatch monks to every city and temple to search and when we find those from the Ruthless Sect, we will restore peace to Buddha’s Country."

"Does Benefactor Jiang Qun doubt that Buddha’s Country can handle the matters of the Ruthless Sect properly?"

Officials who had accompanied Prince Jiang Qun frowned in secret. It seemed that Buddha’s Country considered this an internal affair and was reluctant to involve Great Xia.

If your Buddha’s Country could have managed it well, would our Great Xia Cultivators have discovered any trace of the Ruthless Sect?

Our Great Xia Cultivators arrived a few days ago and uncovered their whereabouts. How is it that Buddha’s Country didn’t notice it for so many years?

Master Mingyu also felt somewhat helpless. The dynasties of the Central Continent had changed three times, yet Buddha’s Country remained unshaken, surely having something to rely on, but this reliance was not to be exposed. Cooperating with Great Xia might risk exposing it.

When negotiating between countries, especially involving deep cooperation, rules must be respected. After learning about the Ruthless Sect, Great Xia sent Prince Jiang Qun as a representative to discuss cooperation.

Regrettably, they encountered obstruction at the very first step.

It was uncertain how the Crown Prince would respond; the officials’ attention was focused on his actions.

The Crown Prince smiled faintly, as if he had anticipated Buddha’s Country’s reaction all along.

It was reasonable to assume that in order to find traces of the Ruthless Sect, Spiritual Sense had to be used for the search. That would mean combing through Buddha’s Country from top to bottom, a prospect nobody would agree to.

"The Ruthless Sect matters, naturally, belong to the internal affairs of Buddha’s Country; it would be overstepping for Great Xia to intervene. Our Great Xia has always been one to follow rules, and we certainly would not do anything to overstep. Therefore, we have come up with a good solution."

He took out a piece of paper from his Storage Ring and had someone deliver it to Master Mingyu.

"This is an application letter written by the Senior Meng Junzi. He has long admired Buddha’s Country and has always wanted to join Buddhism but never found a proper chance. He would like to take this opportunity to join Buddha’s Country, and he hopes Master Mingyu will give him a chance."

"Presumably, with Senior Meng Junzi joining Buddha’s Country to handle the Ruthless Sect affairs, it would no longer be considered an overstepping by Great Xia."

...

"What kind of nonsense is this? Has our entire Meng Family joined Buddha’s Country?" When Meng Jingzhou heard the news, he was dumbfounded, his grip on his spoon faltering, and with a clatter, it fell into his plate, making the vegetarian meal unappetizing.

How could I become a citizen of Buddha’s Country while merely on a trip here?

I’m just here for sightseeing!

"I told you that nickname wasn’t given by mistake; you have a connection with Buddhism, Buddhist Meng." Hearing the news, Lu Yang, who had been sitting on the edge of the bed, started laughing so hard he rolled on the bed.

Having laughed for a while and finally tiring, Lu Yang, with good intentions, patted Meng Jingzhou’s shoulder to distract him: "Alright, alright, stop thinking about joining Buddhism. Go out for a walk to change your mindset."

As the Buddha Bathing Festival drew near, Kaihuang City visibly buzzed with excitement. Commoners and Cultivators from other regions were hurrying towards Kaihuang City, and images of Lu Yang and his companion were even posted at the city gate as part of the promotion.

Although, Lu Yang always felt that it seemed less like advertising and more like a wanted poster.

Regardless, Lu Yang and his companion’s appearances were widely known; venturing outside required a disguise.

Light masks adhered to their faces, they adopted different visages and left Kaihuang Temple.

Chapter 993: 992: Skull Divine Sect!

After Lu Yang and another put on masks, their appearances and auras changed completely, making them inconspicuous in the crowd.

"Two bowls of porridge, please."

Lu Yang was curious about the novelty, as the street chef was cooking something indistinguishable, except that it was a very thick pot bubbling away. Considering that similar shops were everywhere, it must be a special product of Buddha Country.

"Okay."

The chef dished out two scoops of the paste onto rice, which looked utterly unappetizing.

Lu Yang tasted it and was caught between laughter and tears; the flavor was beyond words.

Previously, they had always eaten vegetarian meals at Kaihuang Temple, or they had eaten at Goodman Cui’s, which was quite delicious. This had led him to believe that the culinary standards of Buddha Country were above par.

"I was wondering why no one in Great Xia buys this stuff. Who would eat this?" Meng Jingzhou couldn’t help but comment after tasting a few bites.

If the porridge from Buddha Country was really delicious, its people would have sold it in Great Xia long ago. It hadn’t spread in thirty thousand years, which shows how ordinary the taste of Buddha Country’s porridge is.

At the very least, it didn’t suit the tastes of Great Xia people.

The two were discouraged on their first outing, eating the porridge slowly.

To eat or not to eat? Not eating would waste food, but eating might be even worse than wasting it.

"Old Meng, do you have some magic skill that blocks taste?"

"If I had, I would have used it already."

"The two masters?" a somewhat familiar voice called out, seeming to address Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

The two instinctively looked up and recognized the speaker: "Miss Cui?"

Now that they were disguised, no one could recognize them except for the Cui Family people.

They had previously visited Goodman Cui’s house wearing these very masks.

"It really is the two masters." Cui Queer didn’t expect to encounter Lu Yang and another at such a grimy stall; she had initially thought she was mistaken.

After all, few cultivators would want to come to this sort of place.

"Miss Cui, what brings you here too?"

Cui Queer pointed to a queue not far away holding bowls: "On the fifth, fifteenth, and twenty-fifth, dad always comes here to distribute porridge. Today is the fifth of May."

Lu Yang suddenly understood. No wonder he was called Goodman Cui. He had thought it was because he donated a lot of incense money to Kaihuang Temple, not realizing he really did good deeds.

"By the way, Miss Cui, are you busy later? We’re not familiar with this area, could you please show us around Kaihuang City?" Lu Yang awkwardly asked.

Originally, they had planned to casually stroll around without bothering the monks of Kaihuang Temple, but their first meal out turned out to be unreliable. They couldn’t afford to continue wandering aimlessly and possibly encountering more oddities.

Cui Queer readily agreed: "Sure, but I was actually planning to meet a friend to play. Do you mind if my friend joins us? She quite likes lively atmospheres."

Lu Yang raised his hand and smiled, "That’s perfect, we like lively scenes too."

"Then I’ll go tend to this first and see you later!" Cui Queer noticed that the porridge distribution was getting hectic, and it didn’t seem right to idle there, so she hurried over to help Goodman Cui.

After nearly half an hour, Lu Yang and his companion finally finished the challenging meal, and Cui Queer had also finished her tasks.

"Sorry to keep you waiting, masters."

"The timing is just right. Let’s go, and introduce us to your friend," they responded.

"Let’s go."

Cui Queer, brimming with enthusiasm, led the way. Lu Yang and the other had assumed her friend would be a young mistress from a wealthy family, but to their surprise, she kept leading them deeper into the slums.

Realizing their confusion, Cui Queer turned her head, embarrassed, and explained: "She’s a friend I made when I snuck out as a child."

Lu Yang revealed a strange expression, "Why does this road feel so familiar?"

Finally, Cui Que’er knocked on a household’s door, "Qiaoqiao, I’m here, open the door."

"Sister Queer, you’ve arrived."

The door opened, and it was indeed Tang Qiaoqiao, who had visited the Kaihuang Temple before.

"Who are these two?" Tang Qiaoqiao excitedly opened the door, seeing Lu Yang and another, somewhat surprised.

She remembered that Sister Queer had never brought strangers to their home before.

"Let me introduce them. This is Master Lu, and this is Master Meng," Cui Que’er noticed that Lu Yang and the other were in disguise and probably did not want to attract attention, so she did not introduce their full names. Tang Qiaoqiao, hearing their surnames, showed a surprised expression.

Recalling past events at Sister Queer’s home and all the overwhelming publicity, it was not hard to guess their identities, Little Face’s excitement turned her cheeks red.

"Please come in quickly."

"Brother, we have guests."

"Sister Queer is here, just say Sister Queer is here... oh, there really are guests."

Tang Qiaoqiao secretly nudged Tang Chuanwu twice with her elbow, "These two are real masters, one is Master Lu, the other is Master Meng, be polite when you speak."

Tang Chuanwu’s pupils contracted, probably guessing the identities of the two Lu Yang.

"Hello," Lu Yang pretended it was his first meeting with Tang Chuanwu.

"Hello, my name is Tang Chuanwu." For some reason, Tang Chuanwu felt unexplainably reserved in Lu Yang’s presence, as if his prideful secrets were going to be revealed.

It couldn’t be, it couldn’t be, even the figures of legend couldn’t see through his secrets. The ability to rewind time was known only to him, and only he could preserve his memories during the rewind.

"Are you studying pharmacology?" Meng Jingzhou noticed a book open on the table, listing various common herbs.

"Yes, my brother is an apprentice at a pharmacy," Tang Qiaoqiao proudly introduced her brother.

"Can you read?" Meng Jingzhou was slightly surprised; usually, literacy rates were quite low among children from the slums.

"Sister Queer taught me."

Cui Que’er slapped the back of Tang Chuanwu’s head, "How many times have I told you, call me sister."

Tang Chuanwu displayed a look of disdain, refusing to call her sister.

Lu Yang revealed a knowing smile, it seemed Tang Chuanwu had a crush on Cui Que’er.

That made sense, they had known each other since childhood, Cui Que’er was quite beautiful and kind-hearted, having a crush on her was nothing out of the ordinary.

Just as Lu Yang was about to tease them with a laugh, he suddenly heard a bang. The wooden door shattered, and several mysterious people clad in black robes burst into Tang Qiaoqiao’s home.

The hems of their black robes were painted with a red skull symbol.

Tang Chuanwu recognized the skull symbol, his expression dramatically changed, "People from the Skull Divine Sect!"

The leader let out an inhuman laugh, lifted his hood, and revealed a face swollen as if soaked in water.

"Boy, I am Protector Jiao of the Skull Divine Sect’s Twenty-Eight Constellations. You took something from my sect, did you think hiding it here would keep us from finding it?"

Protector Jiao licked his lips, surveyed Lu Yang and the others, who were just frail mortals.

"You tell me, hand it over willingly and we’ll let you keep a full corpse, or should I slaughter you all?"

Tang Chuanwu inwardly cursed his luck; despite his extreme caution, he had still been found by the Skull Divine Sect.

Cultivators truly possessed all kinds of incredible Divine Abilities!

Lu Yang’s mind was full of questions. What was the Skull Divine Sect, and why had he never heard of it?

Chapter 994: 993: The Fall of the Divine Sect

Tang Chuanwu only regretted that his age prevented him from cultivating; otherwise, he would not have been so passive in the face of cultivators, knowing so little about their methods. His only option was to continue to practice martial arts to strengthen himself.

Three months ago, he left the city to gather herbs. Since the journey to the collection site was so far, requiring a full day for a round trip, herbal apprentices usually stayed at the site for two or three days, and Tang Chuanwu was no exception.

He found an oasis to rest, and the first day passed without incident. However, on the second night, a person stumbled into the oasis, seriously wounded and bleeding profusely, clinging to a wooden box until death, eyeing him warily.

He did not want to stir up trouble and sensed that something significant had occurred, so without regard for the dangers of night travel, he prepared to leave.

But before he could leave, black-robed individuals bearing the mark of the Skull Divine Sect surrounded the oasis. Worried that Tang Chuanwu might leak their secrets, they used magic, causing his bones to grow rapidly within his body, piercing his flesh and heart, and killing him.

In his second reincarnation, Tang Chuanwu waited until the man died, opened the wooden box, and found an unknown elixir and a small fragment. He planned to take the elixir and flee, but it was a step too late—the black-robed individuals surrounded the oasis again, killing him.

In the third reincarnation, Tang Chuanwu immediately swallowed the elixir upon finding it. Agonizing pain sent him into unconsciousness, and while he was out, the black-robed individuals killed him.

In the fourth reincarnation, Tang Chuanwu disregarded the wooden box, planning to escape back to Kaihuang City at dawn. Unfortunately, the black-robed people had already set up an extensive encirclement nearby, preventing anyone from leaving the region. His escape failed, and he died.

In the fifth reincarnation, he broke out of the encirclement only to die in a sudden sandstorm.

The sixth, the seventh...

Tang Chuanwu kept reincarnating until he finally understood the whole story. These black-robed individuals were members of the Skull Divine Sect, all cultivators, their influence spread across the nearby eight cities, terribly fearsome.

Among them, one member coveted the treasures of the Divine Sect, stealing a Marrow Cleansing Pill and a fragment of mysterious origin, exceptionally sharp.

In the last reincarnation, he ambushed the one who had stolen the treasures.

The man was already gravely wounded, and combined with the Long Arm Boxing technique he had pilfered from the Kaihuang Temple, along with the familiarity of combat routines from previous reincarnations, he killed the man and seized the wooden box, swallowed the Marrow Cleansing Pill, and after regaining consciousness, broke through the encirclement.

The Skull Divine Sect’s men wanted to pursue him but were thrown into disarray by the sandstorm.

His body became stronger after the Marrow Cleansing Pill, and having prepared for the sandstorm, he finally escaped with his life.

Unexpectedly, three months later, he was still found by members of the Skull Divine Sect.

This is troublesome. He heard that the Constellation Protectors of the Twenty-Eight Constellations were all major figures at the Consummation of Qi Refinement. A direct confrontation was definitely not an option. He wondered how many times he would have to die before he could deal with Protector Jiao.

Tang Chuanwu’s mind raced with plans for the next reincarnation. The Buddha Bathing Festival would begin in ten days. Many cultivators from other regions were already entering Kaihuang City. Perhaps he could use these foreign cultivators to engage Protector Jiao. However, only cultivators at the Foundation Establishment Stage could fight Protector Jiao, and such cultivators were not easy to find.

Moreover, he heard that the Master of the Skull Divine Sect was a Nascent Soul Elder, whose every move could cause extraordinary phenomena, and who was incredibly powerful, mastering over a hundred magic skills. Even if he got past this obstacle, how would he deal with the Divine Sect Master later on?

Wait, it seems that this time, reincarnation isn’t necessary.

Tang Chuanwu turned his head to look at Master Lu, who was deep in thought, and Master Meng, who wore a puzzled expression.

Cui Que’er and Tang Qiaoqiao looked at Protector Jiao with strange eyes—confusion, curiosity, amusement... but no fear.

The sleeves of the black-robed person continually extended bones, rapidly spreading across the entire room. Sharp bone spurs were aimed at Lu Yang and the others; with just one command, the spurs would proliferate again, stabbing them into a hive!

"What’s the matter, not handing over the treasures yet? It seems you won’t shed tears until you see the coffin," Protector Jiao sneered continuously. After he killed, he would just leave, and even Kaihuang Temple would not trace his whereabouts.

Protector Jiao gave a signal and ordered his Left and Right Protectors, "Do it!"

"Do what? Can’t you see I’m in the middle of thinking?" Meng Jingzhou said impatiently as he stepped forward, ignoring the thorny bone spurs, letting them pierce his body.

Protector Jiao’s pupils shrank. The supposedly indestructible bone spurs broke upon contact, like foxtails, causing no harm whatsoever.

"This is impossible, who are you!" Protector Jiao shouted, sensing something was very wrong. This time he had hit an iron wall!

Meng Jingzhou couldn’t be bothered to talk. He released his imposing aura; with his natural commanding presence, Protector Jiao, and the others felt as if they were carrying the weight of mountains on their backs. They collapsed to the ground with a clang, bleeding from all seven orifices!

"You wouldn’t dare kill me. Mess with the Skull Divine Sect, and even if you run to the ends of the earth, we will kill you!"

"Don’t even think that everyone else is like me; I’m just the weakest of the twenty-eight Protectors!"

"Above the Twenty-Eight Constellations Protector, there are the Seven Luminaries Protector, Four Symbols Protector, Left and Right Protectors, the Holy Daughter and Son, and the mysterious and unfathomable leader of the..."

Lu Yang looked up, his eyes widening in realization, "I was wondering why your clothes looked so familiar. I’ve seen them before."
